[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/web/state/cook-session.ts'
open(p,'w').write('''import type { Progress, ProgressEntry, Step } from '../../core/index.js';
import { clockAt, MINUTE_MS, minuteAt } from './clock.js';
import type { CookCore, StepFact } from './cook-slot.js';

/** A start or end more than this many minutes from the schedule's rebuilds; within it the fact is only recorded. */
export const SLIP_MIN = 2;
/** A step this many minutes past its planned time is shown late (never rebuilt by itself). */
export const OVERDUE_MIN = 5;
/** "Undo" is offered for this long after an action. */
export const UNDO_MS = 10_000;
/** A session whose last action is older asks to resume or discard. */
export const STALE_MS = 8 * 60 * MINUTE_MS;
/** Items in "Up next". */
export const UP_NEXT_COUNT = 3;
/** Build log entries kept, oldest dropped. */
export const LOG_MAX = 20;
/** `cook/tick` interval, for display. */
export const TICK_MS = 15_000;
export const EXTRA_CHOICES = [5, 10, 15] as const;
/** Equals core's `REBUILD_LIMITS.extraMax`, which `src/core/index.ts` does not export (checked in a test). */
export const EXTRA_MAX = 1440;

/**
 * The instant every action records its facts at: the clock, but never before the session began
 * or before any recorded fact, so a clock set backwards cannot make a fact end before it started.
 */
export function effectiveNow(core: CookCore, nowMs: number): number {
  let t = Math.max(nowMs, core.beganAtMs);
  for (const fact of Object.values(core.facts)) {
    t = Math.max(t, fact.startedAtMs);
    if (fact.state === 'done') t = Math.max(t, fact.endedAtMs);
  }
  return t;
}

/** The facts as core `Progress` on the session's axis. */
export function progressOf(core: CookCore): Progress {
  const progress: Progress = {};
  for (const [id, fact] of Object.entries(core.facts)) {
    const startMin = minuteAt(core.axisMs, fact.startedAtMs);
    const entry: ProgressEntry =
      fact.state === 'done'
        ? { state: 'done', startMin, endMin: minuteAt(core.axisMs, fact.endedAtMs) }
        : { state: 'running', startMin, extraMin: fact.extraMin };
    progress[id] = entry;
  }
  return progress;
}

/** When a running step is ready: its recorded start plus its minutes plus the extra minutes. */
export function endsAtMs(step: Step, fact: Extract<StepFact, { state: 'running' }>): number {
  return clockAt(fact.startedAtMs, step.minutes + fact.extraMin);
}
''')
EOF
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner/src/web/state/cook-slot.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""import type {
  HoldExceededIssue,
  RebuildError,
  RebuiltSchedule,
  Schedule,
  StepMove,
} from '../../core/index.js';""","""import {
  IssueSchema,
  RebuiltScheduleSchema,
  StepIdSchema,
  StepMoveSchema,
} from '../../core/index.js';
import type {
  HoldExceededIssue,
  RebuildError,
  RebuiltSchedule,
  Schedule,
  StepMove,
} from '../../core/index.js';
import { EXTRA_MAX } from './cook-session.js';""")
a=s.index("// STUB (T1.1): replaced in T1.2.")
b=s.index("// STUB (T1.1): replaced in T1.8.")
schema='''const ms = z.int().min(0);
const stepId = StepIdSchema;

const HoldExceededSchema = IssueSchema.refine(
  (issue): issue is HoldExceededIssue => issue.code === 'hold_exceeded',
);

const StepFactSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('state', [
  z.strictObject({
    state: z.literal('running'),
    startedAtMs: ms,
    extraMin: z.int().min(0).max(EXTRA_MAX),
  }),
  z.strictObject({ state: z.literal('done'), startedAtMs: ms, endedAtMs: ms }),
]);

const LogCauseSchema = z.union([
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('begin-late'), byMin: z.int() }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.enum(['start', 'done']), stepId, byMin: z.int() }),
  z.strictObject({
    kind: z.literal('extra'),
    stepId,
    minutes: z.union([z.literal(5), z.literal(10), z.literal(15)]),
  }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('behind') }),
]);

const LogResultSchema = z.union([
  z.strictObject({
    ok: z.literal(true),
    moved: z.array(StepMoveSchema),
    unchanged: z.array(stepId),
    servingFromMin: z.int(),
    servingToMin: z.int(),
    newWarnings: z.array(HoldExceededSchema),
  }),
  z.strictObject({
    ok: z.literal(false),
    error: z.enum([
      'invalid_schedule',
      'invalid_now',
      'invalid_progress',
      'unknown_step',
      'done_ends_before_start',
      'done_after_now',
      'running_starts_after_now',
      'serving_out_of_range',
    ]),
  }),
]);

const LogEntrySchema = z.strictObject({ atMs: ms, cause: LogCauseSchema, result: LogResultSchema });

/** Every fact and log id must be a step of the core's own schedule. */
const CookCoreSchema = z
  .strictObject({
    axisMs: z.int(),
    plannedServingMs: z.int(),
    beganAtMs: ms,
    schedule: RebuiltScheduleSchema,
    facts: z.record(z.string(), StepFactSchema),
    log: z.array(LogEntrySchema).max(LOG_MAX),
  })
  .refine((core) => {
    const ids = new Set(core.schedule.steps.map((s) => s.id));
    const known = (id: string): boolean => ids.has(id);
    return (
      Object.keys(core.facts).every(known) &&
      core.log.every((entry) => {
        const { cause, result } = entry;
        const causeOk = 'stepId' in cause ? known(cause.stepId) : true;
        const resultOk = result.ok
          ? result.moved.every((m) => known(m.stepId)) && result.unchanged.every(known)
          : true;
        return causeOk && resultOk;
      })
    );
  });

const UndoLabelSchema = z.union([
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.enum(['start', 'done']), stepId }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('extra'), stepId, minutes: z.int() }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('behind') }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('start-now') }),
]);

/** The undo slot holds a `CookCore`, never a `CookState`, so slots never nest. */
export const CookStateSchema: z.ZodType<CookState> = z.strictObject({
  version: z.literal(1),
  core: CookCoreSchema,
  lastActiveMs: ms,
  alertsWanted: z.boolean(),
  undo: z
    .strictObject({ label: UndoLabelSchema, untilMs: ms, before: CookCoreSchema })
    .nullable(),
});

'''
s=s[:a]+schema+s[b:]
s=s.replace("import { EXTRA_MAX } from './cook-session.js';","import { EXTRA_MAX, LOG_MAX } from './cook-session.js';")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/web/state/cook-session.ts'
s=open(p).read()
a=s.index("export type BeginError")
s=s[:a]
s=s.replace("import { err, ok } from '../../core/index.js';","import { compareIds, err, ok, rebuild, RebuiltScheduleSchema } from '../../core/index.js';")
s=s.replace("import type { CookAction, CookCore, CookState, StepFact } from './cook-slot.js';","import type {\n  CookAction,\n  CookCore,\n  CookState,\n  LogCause,\n  LogEntry,\n  StepFact,\n} from './cook-slot.js';")
s=s.replace("  Progress,\n  ProgressEntry,\n","  Progress,\n  ProgressEntry,\n  Rebuilt,\n")
s+='''export type BeginError = { kind: 'too_late' } | { kind: 'invalid_schedule' };

/** The log entry of one rebuild, against the schedule it replaced. */
function entryFor(atMs: number, cause: LogCause, old: RebuiltSchedule, built: Rebuilt): LogEntry {
  const oldWaits = new Map(old.warnings.map((w) => [w.stepId, w.waitMin]));
  return {
    atMs,
    cause,
    result: {
      ok: true,
      moved: built.diff.moved.toSorted(
        (a, b) => a.toStartMin - b.toStartMin || compareIds(a.stepId, b.stepId),
      ),
      unchanged: built.diff.unchanged,
      servingFromMin: old.servingMin,
      servingToMin: built.schedule.servingMin,
      newWarnings: built.schedule.warnings.filter((w) => {
        const before = oldWaits.get(w.stepId);
        return before === undefined || before < w.waitMin;
      }),
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Rebuilds `core` against its facts at axis minute `n` and appends the log entry. A failed
 * rebuild keeps the schedule and the facts and logs the error kind (CM5.7).
 */
function rebuildCore(core: CookCore, cause: LogCause, t: number): CookCore {
  const n = minuteAt(core.axisMs, t);
  const result = rebuild(core.schedule, progressOf(core), n);
  const entry: LogEntry = result.ok
    ? entryFor(t, cause, core.schedule, result.value)
    : { atMs: t, cause, result: { ok: false, error: result.error.kind } };
  return {
    ...core,
    schedule: result.ok ? result.value.schedule : core.schedule,
    log: [...core.log, entry].slice(-LOG_MAX),
  };
}

function earliestStartMin(schedule: RebuiltSchedule): number {
  return Math.min(...schedule.steps.map((s) => s.startMin));
}

/** The session a cook would begin now, or why not. `too_late`: the rebuild is out of range. */
export function beginSession(
  schedule: Schedule | RebuiltSchedule,
  axisMs: number,
  nowMs: number,
): Result<CookState, BeginError> {
  const parsed = RebuiltScheduleSchema.safeParse(schedule);
  if (!parsed.success) return err({ kind: 'invalid_schedule' });
  const planned = parsed.data;
  let core: CookCore = {
    axisMs,
    plannedServingMs: clockAt(axisMs, planned.servingMin),
    beganAtMs: nowMs,
    schedule: planned,
    facts: {},
    log: [],
  };
  const n = minuteAt(axisMs, nowMs);
  const startMin = earliestStartMin(planned);
  if (n > startMin) {
    const result = rebuild(planned, {}, n);
    if (!result.ok) {
      const kind = result.error.kind;
      return err({
        kind: kind === 'invalid_now' || kind === 'serving_out_of_range' ? 'too_late' : 'invalid_schedule',
      });
    }
    core = {
      ...core,
      schedule: result.value.schedule,
      log: [entryFor(nowMs, { kind: 'begin-late', byMin: n - startMin }, planned, result.value)],
    };
  }
  return ok({ version: 1, core, lastActiveMs: nowMs, alertsWanted: false, undo: null });
}

export function canBegin(
  schedule: Schedule | RebuiltSchedule,
  axisMs: number,
  nowMs: number,
): Result<null, BeginError> {
  const result = beginSession(schedule, axisMs, nowMs);
  return result.ok ? ok(null) : result;
}

type Undoable = { label: UndoLabel; core: CookCore; t: number };

/** The state after an undoable action: the new core, the undo slot holding the old one. */
function undoable(state: CookState, change: Undoable): CookState {
  return {
    ...state,
    core: change.core,
    lastActiveMs: change.t,
    undo: { label: change.label, untilMs: change.t + UNDO_MS, before: state.core },
  };
}

/** "Start now": the minutes until the first start, rounded up so the start is reached. */
export function startNowShiftMin(core: CookCore, nowMs: number): number {
  const startMs = clockAt(core.axisMs, earliestStartMin(core.schedule));
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil((startMs - nowMs) / MINUTE_MS));
}

/** One action on a session. An action that cannot apply returns `state` itself (CM3.7). */
export function applyCook(
  state: CookState,
  action: Exclude<CookAction, { type: 'cook/begin' | 'cook/stop' }>,
): CookState {
  const { core } = state;
  const t = effectiveNow(core, 'nowMs' in action ? action.nowMs : state.lastActiveMs);
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'cook/start-now': {
      const startMs = clockAt(core.axisMs, earliestStartMin(core.schedule));
      if (Object.keys(core.facts).length > 0 || core.log.length > 0 || t >= startMs) return state;
      const shiftMs = startNowShiftMin(core, t) * MINUTE_MS;
      return undoable(state, {
        label: { kind: 'start-now' },
        t,
        core: {
          ...core,
          axisMs: core.axisMs - shiftMs,
          plannedServingMs: core.plannedServingMs - shiftMs,
        },
      });
    }
    default:
      // STUB (T1.3): the other actions arrive with T1.5 to T1.8.
      return { ...state };
  }
}
'''
s=s.replace("  LogEntry,\n  StepFact,","  LogEntry,\n  StepFact,\n  UndoLabel,")
open(p,'w').write(s)

type Step = { at: number } & (
  | { do: 'start' | 'done'; id: string }
  | { do: 'extra'; id: string; minutes: 5 | 10 | 15 }
  | { do: 'behind' }
);

/** Applies the steps in order with `applyCook`. */
function run(state: CookState, ...steps: Step[]): CookState {
  let current = state;
  for (const step of steps) {
    if (step.do === 'behind') {
      current = applyCook(current, { type: 'cook/behind', nowMs: step.at });
    } else if (step.do === 'extra') {
      current = applyCook(current, {
        type: 'cook/extra',
        stepId: step.id,
        minutes: step.minutes,
        nowMs: step.at,
      });
    } else {
      current = applyCook(current, {
        type: step.do === 'start' ? 'cook/start' : 'cook/done',
        stepId: step.id,
        nowMs: step.at,
      });
    }
  }
  return current;
}

/** CM-c up to and including the shred, all on plan: the five taps of the design. */
const ON_PLAN: Step[] = [
  { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
  { do: 'done', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.roast', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  { do: 'start', id: 'greens.shred', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  { do: 'done', id: 'greens.shred', at: cmAt(17, 55) },
];

function lastEntry(state: CookState): LogEntry {
  const entry = state.core.log.at(-1);
  expect(entry).not.toBeUndefined();
  if (entry === undefined) throw new Error('no log entry');
  return entry;
}

describe('CM-c step by step (CM3.1, CM5.1, CM5.3, CM5.5)', () => {
  it('CM5.1 the five on-plan taps record facts and log nothing; the schedule is untouched', () => {
    const start = begun(cmAt(17, 30));
    const after = run(start, ...ON_PLAN);
    expect(after.core.log).toEqual([]);
    expect(after.core.schedule).toBe(start.core.schedule);
    expect(after.core.facts).toEqual(CM_C_FACTS);
    expect(after.lastActiveMs).toBe(cmAt(17, 55));
  });

  it('CM5.1 +15 on the roast at 18:30 logs RB-a: order, unchanged, serving and the one warning', () => {
    const before = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...ON_PLAN);
    const after = run(before, { do: 'extra', id: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15, at: cmAt(18, 30) });
    expect(after.core.facts['chicken.roast']).toEqual({
      state: 'running',
      startedAtMs: cmAt(17, 50),
      extraMin: 15,
    });
    const entry = lastEntry(after);
    expect(entry.atMs).toBe(cmAt(18, 30));
    expect(entry.cause).toEqual({ kind: 'extra', stepId: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15 });
    expect(entry.result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!entry.result.ok) return;
    expect(entry.result.moved).toEqual([
      { stepId: 'chicken.rest', fromStartMin: -25, toStartMin: -10 },
      { stepId: 'gravy.roux', fromStartMin: -20, toStartMin: -4 },
      { stepId: 'gravy.simmer', fromStartMin: -18, toStartMin: -2 },
      { stepId: 'greens.boil', fromStartMin: -10, toStartMin: 3 },
      { stepId: 'greens.toss', fromStartMin: -6, toStartMin: 7 },
      { stepId: 'gravy.strain', fromStartMin: -8, toStartMin: 8 },
      { stepId: 'chicken.carve', fromStartMin: -5, toStartMin: 10 },
    ]);
    expect(entry.result.unchanged).toEqual(['chicken.prep', 'chicken.roast', 'greens.shred']);
    expect([entry.result.servingFromMin, entry.result.servingToMin]).toEqual([0, 15]);
    expect(entry.result.newWarnings.map((w) => [w.stepId, w.waitMin, w.holdMin])).toEqual([
      ['greens.toss', 7, 5],
    ]);
    expect(after.core.schedule.servingMin).toBe(15);
    expect(after.undo?.label).toEqual({ kind: 'extra', stepId: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15 });
    expect(after.undo?.untilMs).toBe(cmAt(18, 30, 10));
    expect(after.undo?.before).toEqual(before.core);
  });
});

describe('CM-d I\'m behind (CM3.2)', () => {
  const FACTS: Step[] = [
    { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
    { do: 'done', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
    { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.roast', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  ];
  it('CM3.2 at 17:53 only the shred moves, to -97; nine steps stay; no serving change', () => {
    const before = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...FACTS);
    expect(before.core.log).toEqual([]);
    const after = run(before, { do: 'behind', at: cmAt(17, 53) });
    const entry = lastEntry(after);
    expect(entry.cause).toEqual({ kind: 'behind' });
    expect(entry.result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!entry.result.ok) return;
    expect(entry.result.moved).toEqual([
      { stepId: 'greens.shred', fromStartMin: -100, toStartMin: -97 },
    ]);
    expect(entry.result.unchanged).toHaveLength(9);
    expect([entry.result.servingFromMin, entry.result.servingToMin]).toEqual([0, 0]);
    expect(entry.result.newWarnings).toEqual([]);
    expect(after.undo?.label).toEqual({ kind: 'behind' });
  });
  it('CM3.2 before any fact, while the countdown runs, behind returns the state itself', () => {
    const state = begun(cmAt(17, 30));
    expect(applyCook(state, { type: 'cook/behind', nowMs: cmAt(17, 35) })).toBe(state);
  });
});

describe('CM-e thresholds (SLIP_MIN 2)', () => {
  const PREP: Step[] = [
    { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
    { do: 'done', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  ];
  it('CM5.1 a start 2 min late (17:52) records the fact and logs nothing', () => {
    const before = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...PREP);
    const after = run(before, { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.roast', at: cmAt(17, 52) });
    expect(after.core.log).toEqual([]);
    expect(after.core.schedule).toBe(before.core.schedule);
    expect(after.core.facts['chicken.roast']).toEqual({
      state: 'running',
      startedAtMs: cmAt(17, 52),
      extraMin: 0,
    });
  });
  it('CM5.1 a start 3 min late (17:53) logs "chicken.roast started 3 min late" and pins the roast at -97', () => {
    const before = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...PREP);
    const after = run(before, { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.roast', at: cmAt(17, 53) });
    expect(after.core.log).toHaveLength(1);
    const entry = lastEntry(after);
    expect(entry.cause).toEqual({ kind: 'start', stepId: 'chicken.roast', byMin: 3 });
    expect(entry.result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!entry.result.ok) return;
    expect(entry.result.moved).toContainEqual({
      stepId: 'chicken.roast',
      fromStartMin: -100,
      toStartMin: -97,
    });
    const roast = after.core.schedule.steps.find((s) => s.id === 'chicken.roast');
    // Pinned at -97 for 75 min: it ends at -22, so rest runs [-22,-2), carve [-2,3), serving 3.
    expect([roast?.startMin, roast?.endMin]).toEqual([-97, -22]);
    expect(after.core.schedule.servingMin).toBe(3);
  });
  it('CM5.1 a done 3 min early (prep at 17:47) logs byMin -3 and moves no start', () => {
    const before = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), {
      do: 'start',
      id: 'chicken.prep',
      at: cmAt(17, 40),
    });
    const after = run(before, { do: 'done', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 47) });
    const entry = lastEntry(after);
    expect(entry.cause).toEqual({ kind: 'done', stepId: 'chicken.prep', byMin: -3 });
    expect(entry.result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!entry.result.ok) return;
    expect(entry.result.moved).toEqual([]);
    expect(entry.result.servingToMin).toBe(0);
    expect(after.core.schedule.servingMin).toBe(0);
  });
  it('CM5.3 an early start (prep at 17:35) shows the pinned step in moved', () => {
    const after = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 35) });
    const entry = lastEntry(after);
    expect(entry.cause).toEqual({ kind: 'start', stepId: 'chicken.prep', byMin: -5 });
    expect(entry.result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!entry.result.ok) return;
    expect(entry.result.moved).toEqual([
      { stepId: 'chicken.prep', fromStartMin: -110, toStartMin: -115 },
    ]);
  });
});

describe('CM3.7 invalid actions return the same object', () => {
  const running = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...ON_PLAN);
  it('start on a running step, start on a done step, start on an unknown id', () => {
    expect(applyCook(running, { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'chicken.roast', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) })).toBe(running);
    expect(applyCook(running, { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'chicken.prep', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) })).toBe(running);
    expect(applyCook(running, { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'ghost.step', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) })).toBe(running);
  });
  it('done on a pending step, on a done step, on an unknown id', () => {
    expect(applyCook(running, { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'chicken.carve', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) })).toBe(running);
    expect(applyCook(running, { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'chicken.prep', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) })).toBe(running);
    expect(applyCook(running, { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'ghost.step', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) })).toBe(running);
  });
  it('extra on a pending step, and extra of 7 minutes', () => {
    expect(
      applyCook(running, { type: 'cook/extra', stepId: 'chicken.carve', minutes: 5, nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) }),
    ).toBe(running);
    const seven = { type: 'cook/extra', stepId: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 7, nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) };
    expect(applyCook(running, seven as unknown as Parameters<typeof applyCook>[1])).toBe(running);
  });
});

describe('extra minutes and the log limit', () => {
  it('CM5.1 extraMin is capped at core REBUILD_LIMITS.extraMax', () => {
    const base = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...ON_PLAN);
    const near: CookState = {
      ...base,
      core: {
        ...base.core,
        facts: {
          ...base.core.facts,
          'chicken.roast': {
            state: 'running',
            startedAtMs: cmAt(17, 50),
            extraMin: REBUILD_LIMITS.extraMax - 5,
          },
        },
      },
    };
    const after = run(near, { do: 'extra', id: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15, at: cmAt(18, 30) });
    expect(after.core.facts['chicken.roast']).toEqual({
      state: 'running',
      startedAtMs: cmAt(17, 50),
      extraMin: REBUILD_LIMITS.extraMax,
    });
  });
  it('CM5.3 the 21st log entry drops the oldest', () => {
    let state = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), {
      do: 'start',
      id: 'chicken.prep',
      at: cmAt(17, 40),
    });
    for (let k = 1; k <= 21; k += 1) {
      state = run(state, { do: 'behind', at: cmAt(17, 40 + k) });
    }
    expect(state.core.log).toHaveLength(20);
    expect(state.core.log[0]?.atMs).toBe(cmAt(17, 42));
    expect(state.core.log.at(-1)?.atMs).toBe(cmAt(18, 1));
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/web/state/cook-session.ts'
s=open(p).read()
a=s.index("/** One action on a session.")
s=s[:a]+'''export type Phase = 'countdown' | 'cooking' | 'finished' | 'stale';

/** Ids of steps no other step lists in `after`. */
export function terminalIdsOf(schedule: RebuiltSchedule): string[] {
  const used = new Set(schedule.plan.steps.flatMap((step) => step.after));
  return schedule.plan.steps.filter((step) => !used.has(step.id)).map((step) => step.id);
}

/** Derived, never stored, so the countdown ends by itself at the start. */
export function phaseOf(state: CookState, nowMs: number): Phase {
  const { core } = state;
  if (terminalIdsOf(core.schedule).every((id) => core.facts[id]?.state === 'done')) {
    return 'finished';
  }
  if (nowMs - state.lastActiveMs > STALE_MS) return 'stale';
  const startMs = clockAt(core.axisMs, earliestStartMin(core.schedule));
  if (Object.keys(core.facts).length === 0 && nowMs < startMs) return 'countdown';
  return 'cooking';
}

const EXTRA_SET: readonly number[] = EXTRA_CHOICES;

/** One action on a session. An action that cannot apply returns `state` itself (CM3.7). */
export function applyCook(
  state: CookState,
  action: Exclude<CookAction, { type: 'cook/begin' | 'cook/stop' }>,
): CookState {
  const { core } = state;
  const t = effectiveNow(core, 'nowMs' in action ? action.nowMs : state.lastActiveMs);
  const n = minuteAt(core.axisMs, t);
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'cook/start-now': {
      const startMs = clockAt(core.axisMs, earliestStartMin(core.schedule));
      if (Object.keys(core.facts).length > 0 || core.log.length > 0 || t >= startMs) return state;
      const shiftMs = startNowShiftMin(core, t) * MINUTE_MS;
      return undoable(state, {
        label: { kind: 'start-now' },
        t,
        core: {
          ...core,
          axisMs: core.axisMs - shiftMs,
          plannedServingMs: core.plannedServingMs - shiftMs,
        },
      });
    }
    case 'cook/start': {
      const row = core.schedule.steps.find((r) => r.id === action.stepId);
      if (row === undefined || core.facts[action.stepId] !== undefined) return state;
      const next: CookCore = {
        ...core,
        facts: { ...core.facts, [action.stepId]: { state: 'running', startedAtMs: t, extraMin: 0 } },
      };
      const byMin = n - row.startMin;
      return undoable(state, {
        label: { kind: 'start', stepId: action.stepId },
        t,
        core:
          Math.abs(byMin) > SLIP_MIN
            ? rebuildCore(next, { kind: 'start', stepId: action.stepId, byMin }, t)
            : next,
      });
    }
    case 'cook/done': {
      const row = core.schedule.steps.find((r) => r.id === action.stepId);
      const fact = core.facts[action.stepId];
      if (row === undefined || fact?.state !== 'running') return state;
      const next: CookCore = {
        ...core,
        facts: {
          ...core.facts,
          [action.stepId]: { state: 'done', startedAtMs: fact.startedAtMs, endedAtMs: t },
        },
      };
      const byMin = n - row.endMin;
      return undoable(state, {
        label: { kind: 'done', stepId: action.stepId },
        t,
        core:
          Math.abs(byMin) > SLIP_MIN
            ? rebuildCore(next, { kind: 'done', stepId: action.stepId, byMin }, t)
            : next,
      });
    }
    case 'cook/extra': {
      const fact = core.facts[action.stepId];
      if (fact?.state !== 'running' || !EXTRA_SET.includes(action.minutes)) return state;
      const extraMin = Math.min(EXTRA_MAX, fact.extraMin + action.minutes);
      if (extraMin === fact.extraMin) return state;
      const next: CookCore = {
        ...core,
        facts: { ...core.facts, [action.stepId]: { ...fact, extraMin } },
      };
      return undoable(state, {
        label: { kind: 'extra', stepId: action.stepId, minutes: action.minutes },
        t,
        core: rebuildCore(
          next,
          { kind: 'extra', stepId: action.stepId, minutes: action.minutes },
          t,
        ),
      });
    }
    case 'cook/behind': {
      if (phaseOf(state, t) !== 'cooking') return state;
      return undoable(state, {
        label: { kind: 'behind' },
        t,
        core: rebuildCore(core, { kind: 'behind' }, t),
      });
    }
    default:
      // STUB (T1.5): undo, tick, resume and alerts arrive with T1.7 and T1.8.
      return { ...state };
  }
}
'''
s=s.replace("  CookState,\n  LogCause,","  CookState,\n  LogCause,")
open(p,'w').write(s)
p='test/web/state/cook-session.test.ts'

if "REBUILD_LIMITS } from" not in t:
    t=t.replace("import { RebuiltScheduleSchema }","import { REBUILD_LIMITS } from '../../../src/core/schema.js';\nimport { RebuiltScheduleSchema }",1)
open(p,'w').write(t)
EOF

describe('CM-h undo (CM3.4, CM5.8)', () => {
  const before = run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...ON_PLAN);
  const extra = (): CookState =>
    run(before, { do: 'extra', id: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15, at: cmAt(18, 30) });

  it('CM3.4 at 18:30:05 the core is the one before the +15: H1, roast extra 0, empty log', () => {
    const after = applyCook(extra(), { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(18, 30, 5) });
    expect(after.core.log).toEqual([]);
    expect(after.core.schedule.servingMin).toBe(0);
    expect(after.core.facts['chicken.roast']).toEqual({
      state: 'running',
      startedAtMs: cmAt(17, 50),
      extraMin: 0,
    });
    expect(after.core).toEqual(before.core);
    expect(after.undo).toBeNull();
    expect(after.alertsWanted).toBe(false);
  });

  it('CM3.7 at 18:30:10 the window is over and undo returns the state itself', () => {
    const state = extra();
    expect(applyCook(state, { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(18, 30, 10) })).toBe(state);
  });

  it('CM3.7 undo with no slot returns the state itself', () => {
    const state = begun(cmAt(17, 30));
    expect(applyCook(state, { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(17, 30, 1) })).toBe(state);
  });

  it('CM3.4 undoing "Start now" restores the old axis', () => {
    const start = begun(cmAt(17, 30));
    const moved = applyCook(start, { type: 'cook/start-now', nowMs: cmAt(17, 30) });
    const back = applyCook(moved, { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(17, 30, 3) });
    expect(back.core.axisMs).toBe(AXIS_MS);
    expect(back.core.plannedServingMs).toBe(AXIS_MS);
  });
});

describe('CM-g overdue is shown, never rebuilt (design review Q4)', () => {
  const cmF = (): CookState => run(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...ON_PLAN);

  it('CM5.1 overdueIds at 19:10:00 (n = -20) are the rest and the roast, sorted', () => {
    expect(overdueIds(cmF().core, cmAt(19, 10))).toEqual(['chicken.rest', 'chicken.roast']);
  });
  it('CM5.1 overdueIds at 19:09:45 (n = -21) are empty', () => {
    expect(overdueIds(cmF().core, cmAt(19, 9, 45))).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('CM5.1 a keeps step within its slack is not overdue; at the end of its slack it is', () => {
    const state = run(
      begun(cmAt(17, 30)),
      { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
      { do: 'done', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
      { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.roast', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
    );
    // The shred planned -100 has slack 75: overdue only from -25 (19:05).
    expect(overdueIds(state.core, cmAt(19, 4, 59))).toEqual([]);
    expect(overdueIds(state.core, cmAt(19, 5))).toEqual(['greens.shred']);
  });
  it('CM5.1 a tick changes nothing, even with steps overdue', () => {
    const state = cmF();
    expect(applyCook(state, { type: 'cook/tick', nowMs: cmAt(19, 10) })).toBe(state);
  });
  it('CM5.1 "I\\'m behind" at 19:10:00 gives the E3 rebuild; a second tap logs "everything is on time"', () => {

    const entry = lastEntry(first);
    expect(entry.cause).toEqual({ kind: 'behind' });
    expect(entry.result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!entry.result.ok) return;
    expect(entry.result.moved).toEqual([
      { stepId: 'chicken.rest', fromStartMin: -25, toStartMin: -20 },
      { stepId: 'gravy.roux', fromStartMin: -20, toStartMin: -15 },
      { stepId: 'gravy.simmer', fromStartMin: -18, toStartMin: -13 },
      { stepId: 'greens.boil', fromStartMin: -10, toStartMin: -5 },
      { stepId: 'gravy.strain', fromStartMin: -8, toStartMin: -3 },
      { stepId: 'greens.toss', fromStartMin: -6, toStartMin: -1 },
      { stepId: 'chicken.carve', fromStartMin: -5, toStartMin: 0 },
    ]);
    expect(entry.result.unchanged).toEqual(['chicken.prep', 'chicken.roast', 'greens.shred']);
    expect([entry.result.servingFromMin, entry.result.servingToMin]).toEqual([0, 5]);
    expect(entry.result.newWarnings).toEqual([]);
    const second = run(first, { do: 'behind', at: cmAt(19, 10) });
    const again = lastEntry(second);
    expect(second.core.log).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(again.result.ok && again.result.moved).toEqual([]);
    expect(again.result.ok && again.result.servingToMin).toBe(5);
  });
});

      do: 'start',

      at: cmAt(17, 40),
    });

    expect(on).toEqual({ ...state, alertsWanted: true });
    expect(applyCook(on, { type: 'cook/alerts', wanted: true })).toBe(on);
  });
});

/**
 * Steps that are late by the design's rule, sorted by id: a running step `OVERDUE_MIN` past its
 * planned end, a pending step `OVERDUE_MIN` (or its slack, if larger) past its planned start.
 * Display only: nothing rebuilds by itself (design review Q4).
 */
export function overdueIds(core: CookCore, nowMs: number): string[] {
  const n = minuteAt(core.axisMs, nowMs);
  const late: string[] = [];
  for (const row of core.schedule.steps) {
    const fact = core.facts[row.id];
    if (fact?.state === 'done') continue;
    const limit =
      fact === undefined
        ? row.startMin + Math.max(OVERDUE_MIN, row.slackMin)
        : row.endMin + OVERDUE_MIN;
    if (n >= limit) late.push(row.id);
  }
  return late.toSorted(compareIds);
}
'''
s=s.replace('''    default:
      // STUB (T1.5): undo, tick, resume and alerts arrive with T1.7 and T1.8.
      return { ...state };
  }''','''    case 'cook/undo': {
      if (state.undo === null || t >= state.undo.untilMs) return state;
      return { ...state, core: state.undo.before, lastActiveMs: t, undo: null };
    }
    case 'cook/tick':
      // Display only: late steps are derived from the clock, so a tick records nothing.
      return state;
    case 'cook/resume':
      return state.lastActiveMs === t ? state : { ...state, lastActiveMs: t };
    case 'cook/alerts':
      return state.alertsWanted === action.wanted ? state : { ...state, alertsWanted: action.wanted };
  }''')

s=s[:a]+'''/**
 * Cooking-mode reducer for plan-ui's `reduce` (R9.9). An action that changes nothing returns
 * `state` itself; `cook/begin` with a session already there, or one that cannot begin, too.
 */
export function reduceCook<S extends { cook: CookState | null }>(
  state: S,
  action: CookAction,
): S {
  if (action.type === 'cook/stop') {
    return state.cook === null ? state : { ...state, cook: null };
  }
  if (action.type === 'cook/begin') {
    if (state.cook !== null) return state;
    const begun = beginSession(action.schedule, action.axisMs, action.nowMs);
    return begun.ok ? { ...state, cook: begun.value } : state;
  }
  if (state.cook === null) return state;
  const next = applyCook(state.cook, action);
  return next === state.cook ? state : { ...state, cook: next };
}
'''
s=s.replace("import { EXTRA_MAX, LOG_MAX } from './cook-session.js';","import { applyCook, beginSession, EXTRA_MAX, LOG_MAX } from './cook-session.js';")
open(p,'w').write(s)

[tool:execute FAILED] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat >> test/support/oracles.ts <<'EOF'

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Cooking-mode oracles (cooking-mode spec). Plain loops over steps; the minute of an instant is
// written out here, not taken from src/web/state/clock.ts.

/** What the cooking oracles need of a schedule: planned starts, ends and slack per step. */
export interface CookOracleSchedule {
  steps: readonly { id: string; startMin: number; endMin: number; slackMin: number }[];
}

/** What the cooking oracles need of a fact: only whether the step is running or done. */
export type CookOracleFacts = Readonly<Record<string, { state: 'running' | 'done' }>>;

const MS_PER_MINUTE = 60_000;

/**
 * CM2.2 to CM2.6: the ids of the steps that are due (pending, planned start at or before now),
 * running, and the first three pending steps that are not due by (planned start, id).
 */
export function oracleCookSections(
  schedule: CookOracleSchedule,
  facts: CookOracleFacts,
  axisMs: number,
  nowMs: number,
): { due: string[]; running: string[]; upNext: string[] } {
  const due: string[] = [];
  const running: string[] = [];
  const later: { id: string; startMs: number }[] = [];
  for (const row of schedule.steps) {
    const fact = facts[row.id];
    if (fact?.state === 'done') continue;
    if (fact?.state === 'running') {
      running.push(row.id);
      continue;
    }
    const startMs = axisMs + row.startMin * MS_PER_MINUTE;
    if (startMs <= nowMs) due.push(row.id);
    else later.push({ id: row.id, startMs });
  }
  later.sort((a, b) => a.startMs - b.startMs || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
  return {
    due: due.sort(),
    running: running.sort(),
    upNext: later.slice(0, 3).map((x) => x.id),
  };
}

/**
 * The late steps, sorted by id. With `n` the floor of the minutes since the axis instant: a
 * running step is late when `n >= endMin + 5`; a pending one when `n >= startMin + max(5, slack)`.
 */
export function oracleOverdue(
  schedule: CookOracleSchedule,
  facts: CookOracleFacts,
  axisMs: number,
  nowMs: number,
): string[] {
  const n = Math.floor((nowMs - axisMs) / MS_PER_MINUTE);
  const late: string[] = [];
  for (const row of schedule.steps) {
    const fact = facts[row.id];
    if (fact?.state === 'done') continue;
    if (fact?.state === 'running') {
      if (n >= row.endMin + 5) late.push(row.id);
    } else if (n >= row.startMin + Math.max(5, row.slackMin)) {
      late.push(row.id);
    }
  }
  return late.sort();
}
EOF

s+='''
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Cooking-mode generators (cooking-mode spec, "Test support").

/** Minute 0 of the cooking-mode properties: Wed 2026-11-11 19:30 London (19:30Z). */
const COOK_AXIS_MS = Date.UTC(2026, 10, 11, 19, 30);

export interface CookBegin {
  schedule: Schedule;
  axisMs: number;
  beginMs: number;
}

/** A schedule, the axis, and a begin time from 30 min before the first start to 30 min after. */
export function arbCookBegin(): fc.Arbitrary<CookBegin> {
  return fc
    .tuple(
      arbScheduled(),
      fc.oneof(
        { weight: 1, arbitrary: fc.constant({ minutes: 0, seconds: 0 }) },
        {
          weight: 2,
          arbitrary: fc.record({
            minutes: fc.integer({ min: -30, max: 30 }),
            seconds: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 59 }),
          }),
        },
      ),
    )
    .map(([sched, offset]) => {
      const first = Math.min(...sched.steps.map((s) => s.startMin));
      return {
        schedule: sched,
        axisMs: COOK_AXIS_MS,
        beginMs: COOK_AXIS_MS + (first + offset.minutes) * 60_000 + offset.seconds * 1000,
      };
    });
}

export type CookIntentKind =
  | 'start'
  | 'done'
  | 'extra'
  | 'behind'
  | 'undo'
  | 'tick'
  | 'start-now'
  | 'alerts'
  | 'resume';

export interface CookIntent {
  advanceSec: number;
  kind: CookIntentKind;
  pick: number;
  minutes: 5 | 10 | 15;
}

/** Up to 60 intents; a quarter advance no time, so several land on one instant. */
export function arbCookIntents(): fc.Arbitrary<CookIntent[]> {
  return fc.array(
    fc.record({
      advanceSec: fc.oneof(
        { weight: 1, arbitrary: fc.constant(0) },
        { weight: 3, arbitrary: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 1800 }) },
      ),
      kind: fc.constantFrom<CookIntentKind>(
        'start',
        'start',
        'start',
        'done',
        'done',
        'done',
        'extra',
        'behind',
        'undo',
        'tick',
        'start-now',
        'alerts',
        'resume',
      ),
      pick: fc.nat({ max: 79 }),
      minutes: fc.constantFrom<5 | 10 | 15>(5, 10, 15),
    }),
    { maxLength: 60 },
  );
}

/** What `cookActionFor` needs of a session: its schedule's step ids and its facts. */
export interface CookActionSource {
  core: {
    schedule: { steps: readonly { id: string }[] };
    facts: Readonly<Record<string, { state: 'running' | 'done' }>>;
  };
}

export type CookActionOut =
  | { type: 'cook/start-now' | 'cook/behind' | 'cook/undo' | 'cook/tick' | 'cook/resume'; nowMs: number }
  | { type: 'cook/start' | 'cook/done'; stepId: string; nowMs: number }
  | { type: 'cook/extra'; stepId: string; minutes: 5 | 10 | 15; nowMs: number }
  | { type: 'cook/alerts'; wanted: boolean };

/**
 * The action an intent stands for: the `pick`-th (modulo) pending id for a start, running id for
 * a done or extra, by id, from the schedule and facts. With none to pick, an id that is not in
 * the schedule, which the reducer must ignore. Never goes through `cookView`.
 */
export function cookActionFor(
  state: CookActionSource,
  nowMs: number,
  intent: CookIntent,
): CookActionOut {
  const ids = state.core.schedule.steps.map((s) => s.id).sort();
  const pending = ids.filter((id) => state.core.facts[id] === undefined);
  const running = ids.filter((id) => state.core.facts[id]?.state === 'running');
  const choose = (list: string[]): string =>
    list.length === 0 ? 'ghost.step' : (list[intent.pick % list.length] ?? 'ghost.step');
  switch (intent.kind) {
    case 'start':
      return { type: 'cook/start', stepId: choose(pending), nowMs };
    case 'done':
      return { type: 'cook/done', stepId: choose(running), nowMs };
    case 'extra':
      return { type: 'cook/extra', stepId: choose(running), minutes: intent.minutes, nowMs };
    case 'alerts':
      return { type: 'cook/alerts', wanted: intent.pick % 2 === 0 };
    case 'behind':
      return { type: 'cook/behind', nowMs };
    case 'undo':
      return { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs };
    case 'tick':
      return { type: 'cook/tick', nowMs };
    case 'start-now':
      return { type: 'cook/start-now', nowMs };
    case 'resume':
      return { type: 'cook/resume', nowMs };
  }
}
'''

open('test/support/cook-replay.ts','w').write('''import { reduceCook } from '../../src/web/state/cook-slot.js';
import type { CookAction, CookState } from '../../src/web/state/cook-slot.js';
import { cookActionFor } from './arbitraries.js';
import type { CookBegin, CookIntent } from './arbitraries.js';

export interface CookTraceEntry {
  action: CookAction;
  before: CookState;
  after: CookState;
  nowMs: number;
}

export interface CookReplay {
  /** The session `cook/begin` made, or null when the begin time was out of range. */
  begun: CookState | null;
  trace: CookTraceEntry[];
}

/** Begins a session and plays the intents with `reduceCook`; time only moves forward. */
export function replayCook(begin: CookBegin, intents: readonly CookIntent[]): CookReplay {
  let state: { cook: CookState | null } = { cook: null };
  state = reduceCook(state, {
    type: 'cook/begin',
    schedule: begin.schedule,
    axisMs: begin.axisMs,
    nowMs: begin.beginMs,
  });
  const begun = state.cook;
  if (begun === null) return { begun: null, trace: [] };
  const trace: CookTraceEntry[] = [];
  let nowMs = begin.beginMs;
  for (const intent of intents) {
    const before = state.cook;
    if (before === null) break;
    nowMs += intent.advanceSec * 1000;
    const action = cookActionFor(before, nowMs, intent);
    state = reduceCook(state, action);
    if (state.cook === null) break;
    trace.push({ action, before, after: state.cook, nowMs });
  }
  return { begun, trace };
}
''')

/** A session begun at `nowMs`; fails the calling test when it cannot begin. */
export function beginAt(schedule: Schedule, axisMs: number, nowMs: number): CookState {
  const result = beginSession(schedule, axisMs, nowMs);
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`could not begin: ${result.error.kind}`);
  return result.value;
}

/** One tap of the cook, for tests that play a session by hand. */
export type CookTap = { at: number } & (
  | { do: 'start' | 'done'; id: string }
  | { do: 'extra'; id: string; minutes: 5 | 10 | 15 }
  | { do: 'behind' }
);

/** Applies the taps in order with `applyCook`. */
export function play(state: CookState, ...taps: CookTap[]): CookState {
  let current = state;
  for (const tap of taps) {
    if (tap.do === 'behind') {
      current = applyCook(current, { type: 'cook/behind', nowMs: tap.at });
    } else if (tap.do === 'extra') {
      current = applyCook(current, {
        type: 'cook/extra',
        stepId: tap.id,
        minutes: tap.minutes,
        nowMs: tap.at,
      });
    } else {
      current = applyCook(current, {
        type: tap.do === 'start' ? 'cook/start' : 'cook/done',
        stepId: tap.id,
        nowMs: tap.at,
      });
    }
  }
  return current;
}

p='src/web/state/cook-view.ts'
s=open(p).read()
a=s.index("// STUB (T2.1)")
s=s[:a]+'''const byStartThenId = <T extends { startMs: number; id: string }>(a: T, b: T): number =>
  a.startMs - b.startMs || compareIds(a.id, b.id);

/**
 * Everything the Cook screen shows, derived from the session and the clock alone (CM2.8).
 * Pure: `nowMs` is an argument and nothing is stored.
 */
export function cookView(state: CookState, nowMs: number): CookView {
  const { core } = state;
  const { schedule } = core;
  const phase = phaseOf(state, nowMs);
  const n = minuteAt(core.axisMs, nowMs);
  const rows = new Map(schedule.steps.map((row) => [row.id, row]));
  const steps = new Map(schedule.plan.steps.map((step) => [step.id, step]));
  const dishNames = new Map(schedule.plan.dishes.map((dish) => [dish.id, dish.name]));
  const late = new Set(overdueIds(core, nowMs));
  const startMsOf = (id: string): number => clockAt(core.axisMs, rows.get(id)?.startMin ?? 0);

  const isDone = (id: string): boolean => core.facts[id]?.state === 'done';
  const pending = schedule.plan.steps.filter((step) => core.facts[step.id] === undefined);
  const due = pending.filter((step) => startMsOf(step.id) <= nowMs);
  const waiting = pending.filter((step) => startMsOf(step.id) > nowMs);
  const runningSteps = schedule.plan.steps.filter((step) => core.facts[step.id]?.state === 'running');

  function card(id: string): CardStep {
    const step = steps.get(id);
    const row = rows.get(id);
    const fact = core.facts[id];
    if (step === undefined || row === undefined) throw new Error(`cookView: no step ${id}`);
    const isRunning = fact?.state === 'running';
    const endsAt = isRunning ? endsAtMs(step, fact) : null;
    const lateBy = late.has(id) ? n - (isRunning ? row.endMin : row.startMin) : null;
    return {
      stepId: id,
      title: step.title,
      detail: step.detail ?? null,
      dishName: dishNames.get(step.dish) ?? step.dish,
      minutes: step.minutes,
      handsOn: step.handsOn,
      plannedStartMs: startMsOf(id),
      state: isRunning ? 'running' : 'due',
      startedAtMs: isRunning ? fact.startedAtMs : null,
      endsAtMs: endsAt,
      remainingMs: endsAt === null ? null : endsAt - nowMs,
      waitsFor: isRunning
        ? []
        : schedule.plan.steps.filter((p) => step.after.includes(p.id) && !isDone(p.id)).map((p) => p.title),
      lateByMin: lateBy,
    };
  }

  const startedAt = (id: string): number => {
    const fact = core.facts[id];
    return fact === undefined ? 0 : fact.startedAtMs;
  };
  const lanes: LaneCard[] = [];
  for (let lane = 1; lane <= schedule.kitchen.cooks; lane += 1) {
    const inLane = (step: { id: string; handsOn: boolean }): boolean =>
      step.handsOn && rows.get(step.id)?.lanes.cook === lane;
    const runningHere = runningSteps
      .filter(inLane)
      .sort((a, b) => startedAt(a.id) - startedAt(b.id) || compareIds(a.id, b.id));
    const dueHere = due
      .filter(inLane)
      .sort((a, b) => startMsOf(a.id) - startMsOf(b.id) || compareIds(a.id, b.id));
    const cards = [...runningHere, ...dueHere].map((step) => card(step.id));
    const nextStep = waiting
      .filter(inLane)
      .sort((a, b) => startMsOf(a.id) - startMsOf(b.id) || compareIds(a.id, b.id))[0];
    lanes.push({
      cookLane: lane,
      current: cards[0] ?? null,
      also: cards.slice(1),
      next:
        cards.length === 0 && nextStep !== undefined
          ? { title: nextStep.title, startMs: startMsOf(nextStep.id) }
          : null,
    });
  }

  const toStart = due
    .filter((step) => !step.handsOn)
    .sort((a, b) => startMsOf(a.id) - startMsOf(b.id) || compareIds(a.id, b.id))
    .map((step) => card(step.id));
  const running = runningSteps
    .filter((step) => !step.handsOn)
    .map((step) => card(step.id))
    .sort((a, b) => (a.endsAtMs ?? 0) - (b.endsAtMs ?? 0) || compareIds(a.stepId, b.stepId));
  const upNext = waiting
    .map((step) => ({ id: step.id, startMs: startMsOf(step.id) }))
    .sort(byStartThenId)
    .slice(0, UP_NEXT_COUNT)
    .map(({ id, startMs }): UpNextItem => {
      const step = steps.get(id);
      return {
        stepId: id,
        title: step?.title ?? id,
        dishName: dishNames.get(step?.dish ?? '') ?? '',
        startMs,
        inMin: Math.ceil((startMs - nowMs) / MINUTE_MS),
      };
    });

  const dishes = schedule.plan.dishes.map((dish): DishState => {
    const own = schedule.plan.steps.filter((step) => step.dish === dish.id);
    const doneCount = own.filter((step) => isDone(step.id)).length;
    let kind: DishState['state'];
    if (doneCount === own.length) kind = { kind: 'done' };
    else if (own.some((step) => core.facts[step.id]?.state === 'running')) kind = { kind: 'cooking' };
    else if (own.some((step) => due.includes(step))) kind = { kind: 'due' };
    else {
      const starts = own.filter((step) => waiting.includes(step)).map((step) => startMsOf(step.id));
      kind = { kind: 'next', startMs: Math.min(...starts) };
    }
    return { dishId: dish.id, name: dish.name, done: doneCount, total: own.length, state: kind };
  });

  const first = lanes[0]?.current ?? null;
  const overrun = running.find((c) => (c.remainingMs ?? 1) <= 0);
  let dock: CookView['dock'] = null;
  if (first !== null) dock = { stepId: first.stepId, action: first.state === 'due' ? 'start' : 'done' };
  else if (overrun !== undefined) dock = { stepId: overrun.stepId, action: 'done' };

  let finished: CookView['finished'] = null;
  if (phase === 'finished') {
    const ends = terminalIdsOf(schedule).flatMap((id) => {
      const fact = core.facts[id];
      return fact?.state === 'done' ? [fact.endedAtMs] : [];
    });
    finished = { servedMs: Math.max(...ends), plannedMs: core.plannedServingMs };
  }

  return {
    phase,
    nowMs,
    servingMs: clockAt(core.axisMs, schedule.servingMin),
    plannedServingMs: core.plannedServingMs,
    startMs: clockAt(core.axisMs, Math.min(...schedule.steps.map((row) => row.startMin))),
    lanes,
    toStart,
    running,
    upNext,
    dishes,
    dock,
    emphasiseBehind: phase === 'cooking' && late.size > 0,
    undo:
      state.undo !== null && nowMs < state.undo.untilMs
        ? { label: state.undo.label, untilMs: state.undo.untilMs }
        : null,
    finished,
    log: [...core.log].reverse(),
  };
}
'''

const viewsAround = (
  state: ReturnType<typeof cmF>,
  action: Parameters<typeof applyCook>[1],
  beforeMs: number,
) => {
  const after = applyCook(state, action);
  return { before: cookView(state, beforeMs), after: cookView(after, beforeMs), state: after };
};

describe('focusAfter (CM9.3), one case per row of the focus rule', () => {
  const begun = () => beginAt(H1_SCHEDULE, AXIS_MS, cmAt(17, 30));

  it('start s: the Done button of s', () => {
    const action = { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'chicken.prep', nowMs: cmAt(17, 40) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(begun(), action, cmAt(17, 40));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({
      kind: 'action',
      stepId: 'chicken.prep',
      action: 'done',
    });
  });
  it('done s: the heading of the card now holding the lane', () => {
    const state = play(begun(), { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) });
    const action = { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'chicken.prep', nowMs: cmAt(17, 50) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(state, action, cmAt(17, 50));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({
      kind: 'heading',
      id: 'card-greens.shred',
    });
  });
  it('done s: with the lane free, the next running card', () => {
    const state = play(
      begun(),
      ...ON_PLAN.slice(0, 4),
    );
    const action = { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'greens.shred', nowMs: cmAt(17, 55) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(state, action, cmAt(17, 55));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({
      kind: 'heading',
      id: 'card-chicken.roast',
    });
  });
  it('done s: with nothing to show, the Do now heading', () => {
    const two = schedule(SUNDAY_ROAST, TWO_COOK_KITCHEN);
    if (!two.ok) throw new Error('no schedule');
    const state = play(
      beginAt(two.value, AXIS_MS, cmAt(17, 40)),
      { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
      { do: 'start', id: 'greens.shred', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
    );
    const action = { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'greens.shred', nowMs: cmAt(17, 45) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(state, action, cmAt(17, 45));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({ kind: 'heading', id: 'do-now' });
  });
  it('extra and behind: focus stays where it is', () => {
    const extra = { type: 'cook/extra', stepId: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 5, nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) } as const;
    const a = viewsAround(cmF(), extra, cmAt(18, 0));
    expect(focusAfter(extra, a.before, a.after)).toBeNull();
    const behind = { type: 'cook/behind', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) } as const;
    const b = viewsAround(cmF(), behind, cmAt(18, 0));
    expect(focusAfter(behind, b.before, b.after)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('undo of an extra: the Done button of the restored running step', () => {
    const extra = play(cmF(), { do: 'extra', id: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15, at: cmAt(18, 30) });
    const action = { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(18, 30, 3) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(extra, action, cmAt(18, 30, 3));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({
      kind: 'action',
      stepId: 'chicken.roast',
      action: 'done',
    });
  });
  it('undo of a start: the Start button of the restored pending step', () => {
    const started = play(begun(), { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) });
    const action = { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(17, 40, 3) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(started, action, cmAt(17, 40, 3));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({
      kind: 'action',
      stepId: 'chicken.prep',
      action: 'start',
    });
  });
  it('undo of I\'m behind: the Do now heading', () => {
    const behind = play(cmF(), { do: 'behind', at: cmAt(18, 0) });
    const action = { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0, 3) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(behind, action, cmAt(18, 0, 3));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({ kind: 'heading', id: 'do-now' });
  });
  it('start-now: the heading of the first lane card', () => {
    const action = { type: 'cook/start-now', nowMs: cmAt(17, 30) } as const;
    const v = viewsAround(begun(), action, cmAt(17, 30));
    expect(focusAfter(action, v.before, v.after)).toEqual({
      kind: 'heading',
      id: 'card-chicken.prep',
    });
  });
  it('tick, alerts and resume: no move', () => {
    const state = cmF();
    const view = cookView(state, cmAt(18, 0));
    for (const action of [
      { type: 'cook/tick', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) },
      { type: 'cook/alerts', wanted: true },
      { type: 'cook/resume', nowMs: cmAt(18, 0) },
    ] as const) {
      expect(focusAfter(action, view, view)).toBeNull();
    }
  });
});

describe('finishedBetween (CM4.2)', () => {
  it('the roast, ready at 19:05, is in (19:04:59, 19:05:00] and not in (19:05:00, 19:06]', () => {
    expect(finishedBetween(cmF(), cmAt(19, 4, 59), cmAt(19, 5))).toEqual(['chicken.roast']);
    expect(finishedBetween(cmF(), cmAt(19, 5), cmAt(19, 6))).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('with 15 extra minutes it is ready at 19:20', () => {
    const extra = play(cmF(), { do: 'extra', id: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15, at: cmAt(18, 30) });
    expect(finishedBetween(extra, cmAt(19, 4), cmAt(19, 6))).toEqual([]);
    expect(finishedBetween(extra, cmAt(19, 19, 59), cmAt(19, 20))).toEqual(['chicken.roast']);
  });
  it('a hands-on step under way is not announced', () => {
    const prep = play(beginAt(H1_SCHEDULE, AXIS_MS, cmAt(17, 30)), {
      do: 'start',
      id: 'chicken.prep',
      at: cmAt(17, 40),
    });
    expect(finishedBetween(prep, cmAt(17, 49, 59), cmAt(17, 50))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('announcementFor (CM5.6, CM9.4)', () => {
  const GB = 'en-GB';
  it('CM-b a session that begins 12 minutes late: 10 steps moved, serving 19:42, 1 new warning', () => {
    const after = beginAt(H1_SCHEDULE, AXIS_MS, cmAt(17, 52));
    expect(announcementFor(null, after, undefined, GB)).toBe(
      'Plan rebuilt: 10 steps moved. Serving is now 19:42. 1 new warning.',
    );
  });
  it('CM-c +15 on the roast: 7 steps moved, serving 19:45, 1 new warning', () => {
    const before = cmF();
    const after = play(before, { do: 'extra', id: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15, at: cmAt(18, 30) });
    expect(announcementFor(before, after, undefined, GB)).toBe(
      'Plan rebuilt: 7 steps moved. Serving is now 19:45. 1 new warning.',
    );
  });
  it('CM-d I\'m behind at 17:53: 1 step moved, serving stays at 19:30', () => {
    const before = play(
      beginAt(H1_SCHEDULE, AXIS_MS, cmAt(17, 30)),
      ...ON_PLAN.slice(0, 3),
    );
    const after = play(before, { do: 'behind', at: cmAt(17, 53) });
    expect(announcementFor(before, after, undefined, GB)).toBe(
      'Plan rebuilt: 1 step moved. Serving stays at 19:30.',
    );
  });
  it('a rebuild that moves nothing says everything is on time', () => {
    const before = cmF();
    const after = play(before, { do: 'behind', at: cmAt(18, 0) });
    expect(announcementFor(before, after, undefined, GB)).toBe(
      'Plan rebuilt: everything is on time.',
    );
  });
  it('a failed rebuild is said once', () => {
    const before = cmF();
    const after = {
      ...before,
      core: {
        ...before.core,
        log: [
          ...before.core.log,
          { atMs: cmAt(18, 0), cause: { kind: 'behind' } as const, result: { ok: false, error: 'serving_out_of_range' } as const },
        ],
      },
    };
    expect(announcementFor(before, after, undefined, GB)).toBe('The plan could not be rebuilt.');
  });
  it('an undo names what was undone', () => {
    const before = play(cmF(), { do: 'extra', id: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15, at: cmAt(18, 30) });
    const after = applyCook(before, { type: 'cook/undo', nowMs: cmAt(18, 30, 3) });
    expect(
      announcementFor(before, after, { kind: 'extra', stepId: 'chicken.roast', minutes: 15 }, GB),
    ).toBe('Undone: +15 min on Roast the chicken.');
    expect(announcementFor(before, after, { kind: 'behind' }, GB)).toBe("Undone: I'm behind.");
  });
  it('the last terminal step done: dinner is served', () => {
    const taps = h1Taps(null);
    const start = beginAt(H1_SCHEDULE, AXIS_MS, cmAt(17, 30));
    const before = play(start, ...taps.slice(0, -1));
    const after = play(before, ...taps.slice(-1));
    expect(announcementFor(before, after, undefined, GB)).toBe(
      'Everything is done. Dinner is served.',
    );
  });
  it('an on-plan start announces nothing, and nor does no change', () => {
    const before = beginAt(H1_SCHEDULE, AXIS_MS, cmAt(17, 30));
    const after = play(before, { do: 'start', id: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) });
    expect(announcementFor(before, after, undefined, GB)).toBeNull();
    expect(announcementFor(after, after, undefined, GB)).toBeNull();
    expect(announcementFor(null, null, undefined, GB)).toBeNull();
  });
});

// Cooking mode announcements (cooking-mode design, "Announcements"). The polite live region
// reads these; countdown ticks are never announced.
export const COOK_ANNOUNCE = {
  rebuildFailed: 'The plan could not be rebuilt.',
  rebuiltOnTime: 'Plan rebuilt: everything is on time.',
  finished: 'Everything is done. Dinner is served.',
} as const;

const plural = (n: number, one: string, many: string): string => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;

/** "Plan rebuilt: 7 steps moved. Serving is now 19:45. 1 new warning." */
export function cookRebuilt(
  movedCount: number,
  servingMoved: boolean,
  servingTime: string,
  newWarningCount: number,
): string {
  const warnings = newWarningCount > 0 ? ` ${plural(newWarningCount, 'new warning', 'new warnings')}.` : '';
  if (movedCount === 0 && !servingMoved) return `${COOK_ANNOUNCE.rebuiltOnTime}${warnings}`;
  const serving = servingMoved ? `Serving is now ${servingTime}.` : `Serving stays at ${servingTime}.`;
  return `Plan rebuilt: ${plural(movedCount, 'step', 'steps')} moved. ${serving}${warnings}`;
}

/** "Undone: +15 min on Roast the chicken." */
export function cookUndone(words: string): string {
  return `Undone: ${words}.`;
}

/** "Time is up: Roast the chicken." (several titles joined with a comma). */
export function cookTimeUp(titles: readonly string[]): string {
  return `Time is up: ${titles.join(', ')}.`;
}

python3 - <<'EOF'
p='~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner/src/web/state/cook-view.ts'
s=open(p).read()
a=s.index("// STUBS (T2.3)")
s=s[:a]+'''/**
 * Where focus goes after an action (CM9.3). Heading ids: `do-now` is the "Do now" h2,
 * `card-<stepId>` a card's h3, `card-lane-<n>` the h3 of a free lane card.
 */
export function focusAfter(action: CookAction, before: CookView, after: CookView): FocusTarget {
  const primaryOf = (stepId: string): FocusTarget => {
    const current = after.lanes.flatMap((lane) => [lane.current, ...lane.also]);
    const due = [...current, ...after.toStart].find(
      (card) => card?.stepId === stepId && card.state === 'due',
    );
    if (due !== undefined) return { kind: 'action', stepId, action: 'start' };
    const running = [...current, ...after.running].find(
      (card) => card?.stepId === stepId && card.state === 'running',
    );
    return running === undefined ? null : { kind: 'action', stepId, action: 'done' };
  };
  const firstCard = (): FocusTarget => {
    const next = after.toStart[0] ?? after.running[0];
    return { kind: 'heading', id: next === undefined ? 'do-now' : `card-${next.stepId}` };
  };
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'cook/start':
      return { kind: 'action', stepId: action.stepId, action: 'done' };
    case 'cook/done': {
      const laneIndex = before.lanes.findIndex((lane) =>
        [lane.current, ...lane.also].some((card) => card?.stepId === action.stepId),
      );
      const held = after.lanes[laneIndex]?.current;
      if (held !== null && held !== undefined) return { kind: 'heading', id: `card-${held.stepId}` };
      return firstCard();
    }
    case 'cook/undo': {
      const label = before.undo?.label;
      if (label === undefined || label.kind === 'behind' || label.kind === 'start-now') {
        return { kind: 'heading', id: 'do-now' };
      }
      return primaryOf(label.stepId) ?? { kind: 'heading', id: 'do-now' };
    }
    case 'cook/start-now': {
      const first = after.lanes[0];
      return {
        kind: 'heading',
        id: first?.current == null ? 'card-lane-1' : `card-${first.current.stepId}`,
      };
    }
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Ids of the hands-free steps that are running and whose time came up in `(fromMs, toMs]`,
 * by end time then id (CM4.2).
 */
export function finishedBetween(state: CookState, fromMs: number, toMs: number): string[] {
  const { core } = state;
  const ended: { id: string; at: number }[] = [];
  for (const step of core.schedule.plan.steps) {
    const fact = core.facts[step.id];
    if (step.handsOn || fact?.state !== 'running') continue;
    const at = endsAtMs(step, fact);
    if (at > fromMs && at <= toMs) ended.push({ id: step.id, at });
  }
  return ended.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at || compareIds(a.id, b.id)).map((e) => e.id);
}

export type Announcement = string | null;

function undoWords(after: CookState, label: UndoLabel): string {
  const title = (id: string): string =>
    after.core.schedule.plan.steps.find((step) => step.id === id)?.title ?? id;
  switch (label.kind) {
    case 'start':
      return `started ${title(label.stepId)}`;
    case 'done':
      return `done ${title(label.stepId)}`;
    case 'extra':
      return `+${label.minutes} min on ${title(label.stepId)}`;
    case 'behind':
      return "I'm behind";
    case 'start-now':
      return 'start now';
  }
}

/**
 * What the polite live region says when `before` becomes `after`: an undo (named by `label`),
 * dinner served, or a rebuild. Anything else, such as an on-plan start, says nothing.
 */
export function announcementFor(
  before: CookState | null,
  after: CookState | null,
  label?: UndoLabel,
  locale?: string,
): Announcement {
  if (after === null) return null;
  if (label !== undefined) return cookUndone(undoWords(after, label));
  const wasServed = before !== null && servedIn(before);
  if (servedIn(after) && !wasServed) return COOK_ANNOUNCE.finished;
  const entry = after.core.log.at(-1);
  if (entry === undefined || entry === before?.core.log.at(-1)) return null;
  if (!entry.result.ok) return COOK_ANNOUNCE.rebuildFailed;
  const { moved, servingFromMin, servingToMin, newWarnings } = entry.result;
  return cookRebuilt(
    moved.length,
    servingToMin !== servingFromMin,
    formatClock(clockAt(after.core.axisMs, servingToMin), locale),
    newWarnings.length,
  );
}

function servedIn(state: CookState): boolean {
  return terminalIdsOf(state.core.schedule).every((id) => state.core.facts[id]?.state === 'done');
}

## Review Q4: no automatic rebuild for late steps

**Code**
- `LogCause` has no `overdue` kind.
- `cook/tick` returns the state itself.
- `overdueIds` only feeds the view. Cards now carry `lateByMin`, and `CookView` carries `emphasiseBehind` (cooking phase and some step late).

**`requirements.md`**
- CM5.1 loses the overdue clause.
- New CM2.9: a late step is shown late, and "I'm behind" is emphasised.

**`design.md`**
- Changed: the `OVERDUE_MIN` row, the `LogCause` type, the `tick` table row, the overdue paragraph, the build-log cause line, CM-g, P-CM5 and P-CM6.
- Added: `lateByMin` and `emphasiseBehind` in the view types, and a late-card row in the copy table.

**`tasks.md`**
- T1.7 and T4.6 reworded for the new CM-g.
- I did not touch T3 or the e2e task text beyond that.

## Decisions I made

- **"Start now" rounds up.** The design said `round`. With `round`, a tap at 17:30:40 would still leave 20 s of countdown. `ceil` makes "Start now" start now and moves the serving time at most 59 s further than the exact gap. The `design.md` table row is updated.
- **The expected new warning after a late begin is only `greens.toss`.** T1.3 also named the roast, but the roast ends when the rest starts, so it waits 0 minutes. That matches the design's hand-worked CM-b and my test. I corrected the T1.3 text.
- **`EXTRA_MAX = 1440` is a local constant, with a test that it equals core's `REBUILD_LIMITS.extraMax`.** Core's public surface is pinned by a test and doesn't export that constant. I left core alone.
- **P-CM7 also allows `invalid_now` as a failed-rebuild kind.** It is the same "past the 24-hour window" case as `serving_out_of_range`, and the property asserts the minute really is above 1440.
- **Reducers ignore bad input.** `applyCook` and `beginSession` return the state unchanged for a non-integer or negative instant, a non-boolean `wanted`, or an unknown action type. Otherwise a wild value could corrupt a stored session.
- **`announcementFor` takes an optional `locale`**, like the other layout functions, so tests can use `en-GB`.
- **`finishedBetween` returns step ids**, not titles.

## Not done or not verified

- T3 and T4 were not started, so the copy tables, wake lock, alerts, the Cook screen and all cooking e2e flows remain.
- The oracle tests in `test/meta/oracles.test.ts` passed on first run. The new oracles had no earlier version to fail against, so I covered that with hand-broken inputs that give a different answer.
- `HoldExceededIssueSchema` isn't exported from core. I used `IssueSchema` with a type-predicate refine in `CookStateSchema`, which works but is indirect.

// Cooking mode (cooking-mode design, "Copy"). Stubs until T3.2.
export const COOK_COPY = {} as Record<string, string>;
export interface LogLine {
  mono: string;
  sentence: string;
}
export interface LogLines {
  cause: LogLine;
  moves: LogLine[];
  unchanged: LogLine | null;
  serving: LogLine | null;
  nothing: LogLine | null;
  failed: LogLine | null;
  upToDate: LogLine[];
}
export interface LogContext {
  axisMs: number;
  titleOf(stepId: string): string;
  locale?: string | undefined;
}
export function cookLogLines(entry: LogEntry, ctx: LogContext): LogLines {
  void entry;
  void ctx;
  void clockAt;
  const empty: LogLine = { mono: '', sentence: '' };
  return { cause: empty, moves: [], unchanged: null, serving: null, nothing: null, failed: null, upToDate: [] };
}
export function cookCountdown(ms: number): string {
  void ms;
  return '';
}
export function cookUndoWords(label: UndoLabel, title: string): string {
  void label;
  void title;
  return '';
}
export type { LogCause };
'''
