[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='test/web/copy.test.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("import * as copy from '../../src/web/copy.js';","""import * as copy from '../../src/web/copy.js';
import { applyCook, beginSession } from '../../src/web/state/cook-session.js';
import type { CookState } from '../../src/web/state/cook-slot.js';
import { RebuiltScheduleSchema } from '../../src/core/index.js';
import { AXIS_MS, cmAt, H1_SCHEDULE, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../support/fixtures.js';""")
s+='''
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Cooking mode (T3.1). Expected strings are copied from the design's hand-worked sessions
// (CM-b, CM-c, CM-d, CM-g), not produced by src/.

const H1 = RebuiltScheduleSchema.parse(H1_SCHEDULE);
const titleOf = (id: string): string => SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.find((s) => s.id === id)?.title ?? id;
const ctx = { axisMs: AXIS_MS, titleOf, locale: 'en-GB' } as const;

function begun(nowMs: number): CookState {
  const result = beginSession(H1, AXIS_MS, nowMs);
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`could not begin: ${result.error.kind}`);
  return result.value;
}

type Tap =
  | { at: number; type: 'cook/start' | 'cook/done'; stepId: string }
  | { at: number; type: 'cook/behind' }
  | { at: number; type: 'cook/extra'; stepId: string; minutes: 5 | 10 | 15 };

function tap(state: CookState, ...taps: Tap[]): CookState {
  return taps.reduce((current, t) => {
    if (t.type === 'cook/behind') return applyCook(current, { type: t.type, nowMs: t.at });
    if (t.type === 'cook/extra')
      return applyCook(current, {
        type: t.type,
        stepId: t.stepId,
        minutes: t.minutes,
        nowMs: t.at,
      });
    return applyCook(current, { type: t.type, stepId: t.stepId, nowMs: t.at });
  }, state);
}

const CM_F_TAPS: Tap[] = [
  { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
  { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'chicken.roast', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'greens.shred', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
  { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'greens.shred', at: cmAt(17, 55) },
];

function linesOf(state: CookState): copy.LogLines {
  const entry = state.core.log.at(-1);
  if (entry === undefined) throw new Error('no log entry');
  return copy.cookLogLines(entry, ctx);
}

describe('cooking copy: build log lines (CM5.3, CM5.4)', () => {
  it('CM5.3 CM-b: the cause, ten moves in order, the serve line, no unchanged line', () => {
    const lines = linesOf(begun(cmAt(17, 52)));
    expect(lines.cause).toEqual({
      mono: '17:52 rebuild: started 12 min late',
      sentence: 'Rebuilt at 17:52: started 12 min late.',
    });
    expect(lines.moves.map((m) => m.mono)).toEqual([
      'chicken.prep 17:40 -> 17:52',
      'chicken.roast 17:50 -> 18:02',
      'greens.shred 17:50 -> 18:02',
      'chicken.rest 19:05 -> 19:17',
      'gravy.roux 19:10 -> 19:23',
      'gravy.simmer 19:12 -> 19:25',
      'greens.boil 19:20 -> 19:30',
      'greens.toss 19:24 -> 19:34',
      'gravy.strain 19:22 -> 19:35',
      'chicken.carve 19:25 -> 19:37',
    ]);
    expect(lines.moves[0]?.sentence).toBe('Season and truss the chicken moved from 17:40 to 17:52.');
    expect(lines.unchanged).toBeNull();
    expect(lines.serving).toEqual({
      mono: 'serve 19:30 -> 19:42',
      sentence: 'Serving moved from 19:30 to 19:42.',
    });
    expect(lines.nothing).toBeNull();
    expect(lines.failed).toBeNull();
  });

  it('CM5.3 CM-c: the +15 on the roast, seven moves, three up to date, serve 19:30 -> 19:45', () => {
    const state = tap(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...CM_F_TAPS, {
      type: 'cook/extra',
      stepId: 'chicken.roast',
      minutes: 15,
      at: cmAt(18, 30),
    });
    const lines = linesOf(state);
    expect(lines.cause.mono).toBe('18:30 rebuild: chicken.roast needs 15 more min');
    expect(lines.cause.sentence).toBe('Rebuilt at 18:30: Roast the chicken needs 15 more min.');
    expect(lines.moves.map((m) => m.mono)).toEqual([
      'chicken.rest 19:05 -> 19:20',
      'gravy.roux 19:10 -> 19:26',
      'gravy.simmer 19:12 -> 19:28',
      'greens.boil 19:20 -> 19:33',
      'greens.toss 19:24 -> 19:37',
      'gravy.strain 19:22 -> 19:38',
      'chicken.carve 19:25 -> 19:40',
    ]);
    expect(lines.moves[0]?.sentence).toBe('Rest the chicken under foil moved from 19:05 to 19:20.');
    expect(lines.unchanged?.mono).toBe('3 steps up to date');
    expect(lines.serving?.mono).toBe('serve 19:30 -> 19:45');
    expect(lines.upToDate.map((l) => l.mono)).toEqual([
      'chicken.prep is up to date',
      'chicken.roast is up to date',
      'greens.shred is up to date',
    ]);
    expect(lines.upToDate[1]?.sentence).toBe('Roast the chicken is up to date.');
  });

  it("CM5.3 CM-d: I'm behind moves the shred only; nine up to date; no serve line", () => {
    const state = tap(
      begun(cmAt(17, 30)),
      { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 40) },
      { type: 'cook/done', stepId: 'chicken.prep', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
      { type: 'cook/start', stepId: 'chicken.roast', at: cmAt(17, 50) },
      { type: 'cook/behind', at: cmAt(17, 53) },
    );
    const lines = linesOf(state);
    expect(lines.cause).toEqual({
      mono: "17:53 rebuild: I'm behind",
      sentence: "Rebuilt at 17:53: I'm behind.",
    });
    expect(lines.moves.map((m) => m.mono)).toEqual(['greens.shred 17:50 -> 17:53']);
    expect(lines.unchanged?.mono).toBe('9 steps up to date');
    expect(lines.serving).toBeNull();
    expect(lines.nothing).toBeNull();
  });

  it("CM5.3 CM-g: I'm behind at 19:10 moves seven steps, three up to date, serve 19:30 -> 19:35; a second tap says Everything is on time", () => {
    const first = tap(begun(cmAt(17, 30)), ...CM_F_TAPS, { type: 'cook/behind', at: cmAt(19, 10) });
    const lines = linesOf(first);
    expect(lines.moves.map((m) => m.mono)).toEqual([
      'chicken.rest 19:05 -> 19:10',
      'gravy.roux 19:10 -> 19:15',
      'gravy.simmer 19:12 -> 19:17',
      'greens.boil 19:20 -> 19:25',
      'gravy.strain 19:22 -> 19:27',
      'greens.toss 19:24 -> 19:29',
      'chicken.carve 19:25 -> 19:30',
    ]);
    expect(lines.unchanged?.mono).toBe('3 steps up to date');
    expect(lines.serving?.mono).toBe('serve 19:30 -> 19:35');
    const second = linesOf(tap(first, { type: 'cook/behind', at: cmAt(19, 10) }));
    expect(second.nothing).toEqual({ mono: 'Everything is on time', sentence: 'Everything is on time.' });
    expect(second.moves).toEqual([]);
    expect(second.unchanged?.mono).toBe('10 steps up to date');
    expect(second.serving).toBeNull();
  });

  it('CM5.3 start and done causes name the step and say late or early; one step reads in the singular', () => {
    const at = cmAt(17, 53);
    const base = { atMs: at, result: { ok: true as const, moved: [], unchanged: ['a.b'], servingFromMin: 0, servingToMin: 0, newWarnings: [] } };
    const line = (cause: copy.LogCause): string => copy.cookLogLines({ ...base, cause }, ctx).cause.mono;
    expect(line({ kind: 'start', stepId: 'chicken.roast', byMin: 3 })).toBe('17:53 rebuild: chicken.roast started 3 min late');
    expect(line({ kind: 'done', stepId: 'chicken.prep', byMin: -3 })).toBe('17:53 rebuild: chicken.prep done 3 min early');
    expect(copy.cookLogLines({ ...base, cause: { kind: 'behind' } }, ctx).unchanged?.mono).toBe('1 step up to date');
  });

  it('CM5.7 a failed rebuild says so and that the plan is unchanged', () => {
    const lines = copy.cookLogLines(
      { atMs: cmAt(17, 53), cause: { kind: 'behind' }, result: { ok: false, error: 'serving_out_of_range' } },
      ctx,
    );
    expect(lines.failed).toEqual({
      mono: 'rebuild failed: serving_out_of_range',
      sentence:
        "The plan could not be rebuilt, so it is unchanged. Carry on by the times shown and tap I'm behind to try again.",
    });
    expect(lines.moves).toEqual([]);
    expect(lines.nothing).toBeNull();
  });

  it('CM5.5 CM-c new warning: the hold sentence names the dish, the step and serving', () => {
    expect(copy.holdWarning('Spring greens', 'Toss the greens with butter', 7, 5, null)).toBe(
      'Spring greens: \\u201cToss the greens with butter\\u201d will be ready 7 min before serving and can wait only 5 min. More ovens, burners or cooks may help.',
    );
  });
});

describe('cooking copy: countdowns and sentences (CM1.2, CM2.3, CM2.9, CM4.4, CM8.1)', () => {
  it('CM4.1 countdowns read mm:ss with unbounded minutes and +mm:ss over', () => {
    expect(copy.cookCountdown(600_000)).toBe('10:00');
    expect(copy.cookCountdown(69 * 60_000)).toBe('69:00');
    expect(copy.cookCountdown(-130_000)).toBe('+02:10');
    expect(copy.cookCountdown(500)).toBe('00:01');
    expect(copy.cookCountdown(0)).toBe('00:00');
  });

  it('CM1.2 the countdown screen: heading, first steps, start-now sentence', () => {
    expect(copy.cookStartsAt('17:40')).toBe('Cooking starts at 17:40');
    expect(copy.cookFirst([{ title: 'Season and truss the chicken', dish: 'Roast chicken' }])).toBe(
      'First: Season and truss the chicken (Roast chicken)',
    );
    expect(
      copy.cookFirst([
        { title: 'Season and truss the chicken', dish: 'Roast chicken' },
        { title: 'Shred the greens', dish: 'Spring greens' },
      ]),
    ).toBe('First: Season and truss the chicken (Roast chicken); Shred the greens (Spring greens)');
    expect(copy.cookStartNow('19:20', 10)).toBe('Start now to serve at 19:20, 10 min earlier.');
  });

  it('CM2.3 card lines: due, due since, ready, waits, next, late', () => {
    expect(copy.cookDue('Roast chicken', 10, '17:40', false)).toBe('Roast chicken \\u00b7 10 min \\u00b7 due 17:40');
    expect(copy.cookDue('Roast chicken', 75, '17:50', true)).toBe('Roast chicken \\u00b7 1 h 15 min \\u00b7 due since 17:50');
    expect(copy.cookReady('Roast chicken', '19:05')).toBe('Roast chicken \\u00b7 ready at 19:05');
    expect(copy.cookWaitsFor(['Roast the chicken'])).toBe('Waits for \\u201cRoast the chicken\\u201d, not done yet.');
    expect(copy.cookWaitsFor(['A', 'B'])).toBe('Waits for \\u201cA\\u201d, \\u201cB\\u201d, not done yet.');
    expect(copy.cookNext('Make a roux in the roasting tin', '19:26', 56)).toBe(
      'Next: Make a roux in the roasting tin at 19:26, in 56 min.',
    );
    expect(copy.cookIn(69)).toBe('in 1 h 9 min');
    expect(copy.cookLate(5)).toBe('Late by 5 min');
    expect(copy.cookBlockedAsk('Make a roux in the roasting tin', ['Roast the chicken'])).toBe(
      'Make a roux in the roasting tin waits for \\u201cRoast the chicken\\u201d, not done yet. Start it anyway?',
    );
  });

  it('CM2.7 dish chips', () => {
    expect(copy.cookDishChip('Roast chicken', 1, 4)).toBe('Roast chicken: 1 of 4 done');
    expect(copy.cookDishState('done', '')).toBe('Done');
    expect(copy.cookDishState('cooking', '')).toBe('Cooking');
    expect(copy.cookDishState('due', '')).toBe('Due now');
    expect(copy.cookDishState('next', '19:10')).toBe('Next at 19:10');
  });

  it('CM5.4 the earlier-rebuilds and up-to-date toggles', () => {
    expect(copy.cookEarlier(3)).toBe('Earlier rebuilds (3)');
    expect(copy.cookShowUpToDate(3)).toBe('Show the 3 steps that are up to date');
    expect(copy.cookShowUpToDate(1)).toBe('Show the 1 step that is up to date');
    expect(copy.COOK_COPY['hideUpToDate']).toBe('Hide the steps that are up to date');
    expect(copy.COOK_COPY['logEmpty']).toBe('No rebuilds yet.');
  });

  it('CM3.4 undo buttons and their words', () => {
    const roast = 'Roast the chicken';
    expect(copy.cookUndoButton(copy.cookUndoWords({ kind: 'extra', stepId: 'x', minutes: 15 }, roast))).toBe('Undo: +15 min on Roast the chicken');
    expect(copy.cookUndoButton(copy.cookUndoWords({ kind: 'start', stepId: 'x' }, roast))).toBe('Undo: started Roast the chicken');
    expect(copy.cookUndoButton(copy.cookUndoWords({ kind: 'done', stepId: 'x' }, roast))).toBe('Undo: done Roast the chicken');
    expect(copy.cookUndoButton(copy.cookUndoWords({ kind: 'behind' }, ''))).toBe("Undo: I'm behind");
    expect(copy.cookUndoButton(copy.cookUndoWords({ kind: 'start-now' }, ''))).toBe('Undo: start now');
  });

  it('CM8.1 the finished line: on time, later, earlier', () => {
    expect(copy.cookFinishedLine('19:30', '19:30', 0)).toBe('Served at 19:30. Planned for 19:30: on time.');
    expect(copy.cookFinishedLine('19:33', '19:30', 3)).toBe('Served at 19:33. Planned for 19:30: 3 min later.');
    expect(copy.cookFinishedLine('19:27', '19:30', -3)).toBe('Served at 19:27. Planned for 19:30: 3 min earlier.');
  });

  it('CM7.3 CM1.5 the stale and too-late sentences', () => {
    expect(copy.cookStaleBody('Sunday roast', 'Wed', '17:40')).toBe(
      'You started cooking Sunday roast on Wed at 17:40, more than 8 hours ago.',
    );
    expect(copy.cookTooLate('19:30', 'Fri 13 Nov')).toBe(
      'This plan was for 19:30 Fri 13 Nov, too long ago to cook against. Choose a new serving time on the Plan screen.',
    );
    expect(copy.cookTooLate('19:30', null)).toBe(
      'This plan was for 19:30, too long ago to cook against. Choose a new serving time on the Plan screen.',
    );
  });

  it('CM4.4 the alerts explanation: off, on, after a reload, and without vibration', () => {
    expect(copy.cookAlertsText('off', true)).toBe(
      'Turn on to hear a chime and feel a buzz when a timer ends. The browser allows sound only after you tap here.',
    );
    expect(copy.cookAlertsText('on', true)).toBe(
      'A chime and a buzz play when a timer ends. Silent mode may still mute them.',
    );
    expect(copy.cookAlertsText('reload', true)).toBe(
      'Sound was on before the page reloaded. Tap once to turn it on again.',
    );
    expect(copy.cookAlertsText('off', false)).toBe(
      'Turn on to hear a chime and feel a buzz when a timer ends. The browser allows sound only after you tap here. This device cannot vibrate.',
    );
  });

  it('CM9.4 announcements: rebuild, failed, time up, start reached, finished', () => {
    expect(copy.cookRebuilt(10, true, '19:42', 1)).toBe('Plan rebuilt: 10 steps moved. Serving is now 19:42. 1 new warning.');
    expect(copy.cookRebuilt(7, true, '19:45', 0)).toBe('Plan rebuilt: 7 steps moved. Serving is now 19:45.');
    expect(copy.cookRebuilt(1, false, '19:30', 0)).toBe('Plan rebuilt: 1 step moved. Serving stays at 19:30.');
    expect(copy.cookRebuilt(0, false, '19:30', 0)).toBe('Plan rebuilt: everything is on time.');
    expect(copy.COOK_ANNOUNCE.rebuildFailed).toBe('The plan could not be rebuilt.');
    expect(copy.cookTimeUp(['Roast the chicken'])).toBe('Time is up: Roast the chicken.');
    expect(copy.cookStartReached(['Roast the chicken', 'Shred the greens'])).toBe('Time to start: Roast the chicken, Shred the greens.');
    expect(copy.COOK_ANNOUNCE.finished).toBe('Everything is done. Dinner is served.');
    expect(copy.cookUndone('+15 min on Roast the chicken')).toBe('Undone: +15 min on Roast the chicken.');
  });

  it('CM8.3 CM7.3 the fixed strings of the screens', () => {
    const c = copy.COOK_COPY;
    expect([c['h1'], c['doNow'], c['running'], c['upNext'], c['dishes'], c['buildLog'], c['soundAndScreen']]).toEqual(['Right now', 'Do now', 'Running', 'Up next', 'Dishes', 'Build log', 'Sound and screen']);
    expect([c['start'], c['done'], c['startNow'], c['behind'], c['needsMoreTime']]).toEqual(['Start', 'Done', 'Start now', "I'm behind", 'Needs more time']);
    expect([c['startThenLeave'], c['nothingToDo'], c['nothingMore'], c['alsoDue'], c['timeIsUp'], c['timeLeft'], c['countdownLabel']]).toEqual(['Start, then leave it', 'Nothing to do now', 'Nothing more for this cook.', 'Also due', 'Time is up', 'Time left', 'Time until cooking starts']);
    expect([c['startAnyway'], c['notYet'], c['nothingElse'], c['onTime']]).toEqual(['Start anyway', 'Not yet', 'Nothing else is planned.', 'Everything is on time']);
    expect([c['finishedHeading'], c['backToPlan'], c['staleHeading'], c['resume'], c['discard'], c['discardAsk'], c['discardYes'], c['stopCooking'], c['stopAsk'], c['stopYes'], c['keepCooking'], c['goToPlan']]).toEqual([

  it('CM9.2 plan-ui copy checks over the new strings: no exclamation mark, no task or job, no emoji, no key marker', () => {

s=s[:i]+r'''// Cooking mode (cooking-mode design, "Copy"). Marks such as `!` and `✓` are drawn by the
// components next to these words; the strings here carry the words only.
export const COOK_COPY = {
  documentTitle: 'Cook',
  h1: 'Right now',
  doNow: 'Do now',
  running: 'Running',
  upNext: 'Up next',
  dishes: 'Dishes',
  buildLog: 'Build log',
  soundAndScreen: 'Sound and screen',
  countdownLabel: 'Time until cooking starts',
  timeLeft: 'Time left',
  timeIsUp: 'Time is up',
  start: 'Start',
  done: 'Done',
  startNow: 'Start now',
  behind: "I'm behind",
  needsMoreTime: 'Needs more time',
  startThenLeave: 'Start, then leave it',
  alsoDue: 'Also due',
  nothingToDo: 'Nothing to do now',
  nothingMore: 'Nothing more for this cook.',
  startAnyway: 'Start anyway',
  notYet: 'Not yet',
  nothingElse: 'Nothing else is planned.',
  logEmpty: 'No rebuilds yet.',
  onTime: 'Everything is on time',
  hideUpToDate: 'Hide the steps that are up to date',
  alertsButton: 'Sound and vibration',
  alertsOff: 'Off',
  alertsOn: 'On',
  wakeNote:
    'The screen may turn off while you cook. To keep it on, set Auto-Lock or Screen timeout to a longer time in your device settings.',
  finishedHeading: 'Dinner is served',
  backToPlan: 'Back to the plan',
  staleHeading: 'Carry on with this dinner?',
  resume: 'Resume',
  discard: 'Discard',
  discardAsk: 'Discard the recorded times?',
  discardYes: 'Yes, discard',
  stopCooking: 'Stop cooking',
  stopAsk: 'Stop cooking? The times you recorded will be cleared.',
  stopYes: 'Yes, stop cooking',
  keepCooking: 'Keep cooking',
  goToPlan: 'Go to the plan',
  storedBad: 'Your cooking session could not be read, so it was cleared. Your dinner is still here.',
} as const;

export type { LogCause };

/** A build log line: the mono text shown, and the sentence read by screen readers (ui.md). */
export interface LogLine {
  mono: string;
  sentence: string;
}
export interface LogLines {
  cause: LogLine;
  moves: LogLine[];
  unchanged: LogLine | null;
  serving: LogLine | null;
  nothing: LogLine | null;
  failed: LogLine | null;
  upToDate: LogLine[];
}
export interface LogContext {
  axisMs: number;
  titleOf(stepId: string): string;
  locale?: string | undefined;
}

function causeWords(cause: LogCause, name: (id: string) => string): string {
  switch (cause.kind) {
    case 'begin-late':
      return `started ${formatDuration(cause.byMin)} late`;
    case 'start':
    case 'done': {
      const verb = cause.kind === 'start' ? 'started' : 'done';
      const how = cause.byMin >= 0 ? 'late' : 'early';
      return `${name(cause.stepId)} ${verb} ${formatDuration(Math.abs(cause.byMin))} ${how}`;
    }
    case 'extra':
      return `${name(cause.stepId)} needs ${cause.minutes} more min`;
    case 'behind':
      return COOK_COPY.behind;
  }
}

/** Every line of one build log entry (design, "Build log lines"). */
export function cookLogLines(entry: LogEntry, ctx: LogContext): LogLines {
  const at = formatClock(entry.atMs, ctx.locale);
  const time = (min: number): string => formatClock(clockAt(ctx.axisMs, min), ctx.locale);
  const cause: LogLine = {
    mono: `${at} rebuild: ${causeWords(entry.cause, (id) => id)}`,
    sentence: `Rebuilt at ${at}: ${causeWords(entry.cause, ctx.titleOf)}.`,
  };
  const lines: LogLines = {
    cause,
    moves: [],
    unchanged: null,
    serving: null,
    nothing: null,
    failed: null,
    upToDate: [],
  };
  const { result } = entry;
  if (!result.ok) {
    lines.failed = {
      mono: `rebuild failed: ${result.error}`,
      sentence:
        "The plan could not be rebuilt, so it is unchanged. Carry on by the times shown and tap I'm behind to try again.",
    };
    return lines;
  }
  lines.moves = result.moved.map((move) => {
    const from = time(move.fromStartMin);
    const to = time(move.toStartMin);
    return {
      mono: `${move.stepId} ${from} -> ${to}`,
      sentence: `${ctx.titleOf(move.stepId)} moved from ${from} to ${to}.`,
    };
  });
  if (result.unchanged.length > 0) {
    const text = `${plural(result.unchanged.length, 'step', 'steps')} up to date`;
    lines.unchanged = { mono: text, sentence: `${text}.` };
  }
  const servingMoved = result.servingToMin !== result.servingFromMin;
  if (servingMoved) {
    const from = time(result.servingFromMin);
    const to = time(result.servingToMin);
    lines.serving = {
      mono: `serve ${from} -> ${to}`,
      sentence: `Serving moved from ${from} to ${to}.`,
    };
  }
  if (result.moved.length === 0 && !servingMoved) {
    lines.nothing = { mono: COOK_COPY.onTime, sentence: `${COOK_COPY.onTime}.` };
  }
  lines.upToDate = result.unchanged.map((id) => ({
    mono: `${id} is up to date`,
    sentence: `${ctx.titleOf(id)} is up to date.`,
  }));
  return lines;
}

/** "10:00", "69:00", and "+02:10" once over. Whole seconds, rounded up. */
export function cookCountdown(ms: number): string {
  const seconds = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
  const abs = Math.abs(seconds);
  const pad = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  const text = `${pad(Math.floor(abs / 60))}:${pad(abs % 60)}`;
  return seconds < 0 ? `+${text}` : text;
}

/** The words of an undo, used by the Undo button and the "Undone" announcement. */
export function cookUndoWords(label: UndoLabel, title: string): string {
  switch (label.kind) {
    case 'start':
      return `started ${title}`;
    case 'done':
      return `done ${title}`;
    case 'extra':
      return `+${label.minutes} min on ${title}`;
    case 'behind':
      return COOK_COPY.behind;
    case 'start-now':
      return 'start now';
  }
}
export const cookUndoButton = (words: string): string => `Undo: ${words}`;

export const cookStartsAt = (time: string): string => `Cooking starts at ${time}`;
export const cookPlannedFor = (time: string): string => `Planned for ${time}`;
export function cookFirst(items: readonly { title: string; dish: string }[]): string {
  return `First: ${items.map((item) => `${item.title} (${item.dish})`).join('; ')}`;
}
export function cookStartNow(time: string, minutes: number): string {
  return `Start now to serve at ${time}, ${formatDuration(minutes)} earlier.`;
}

export function cookDue(dish: string, minutes: number, time: string, since: boolean): string {
  return `${dish} \u00b7 ${formatDuration(minutes)} \u00b7 due ${since ? 'since ' : ''}${time}`;
}
export const cookReady = (dish: string, time: string): string => `${dish} \u00b7 ready at ${time}`;
const quoted = (titles: readonly string[]): string =>
  titles.map((title) => `\u201c${title}\u201d`).join(', ');
export const cookWaitsFor = (titles: readonly string[]): string =>
  `Waits for ${quoted(titles)}, not done yet.`;
export function cookNext(title: string, time: string, inMin: number): string {
  return `Next: ${title} at ${time}, in ${formatDuration(inMin)}.`;
}
export const cookLate = (minutes: number): string => `Late by ${formatDuration(minutes)}`;
export const cookIn = (minutes: number): string => `in ${formatDuration(minutes)}`;
export const cookExtra = (minutes: number): string => `+${minutes} min`;
export function cookBlockedAsk(title: string, titles: readonly string[]): string {
  return `${title} waits for ${quoted(titles)}, not done yet. Start it anyway?`;
}

export const cookDishChip = (name: string, done: number, total: number): string =>
  `${name}: ${done} of ${total} done`;
export function cookDishState(kind: 'done' | 'cooking' | 'due' | 'next', time: string): string {
  switch (kind) {
    case 'done':
      return 'Done';
    case 'cooking':
      return 'Cooking';
    case 'due':
      return 'Due now';
    case 'next':
      return `Next at ${time}`;
  }
}

export const cookEarlier = (n: number): string => `Earlier rebuilds (${n})`;
export const cookShowUpToDate = (n: number): string =>
  `Show the ${n} ${n === 1 ? 'step that is' : 'steps that are'} up to date`;

/** `lateMin` is served minus planned: 0 on time, positive later, negative earlier. */
export function cookFinishedLine(served: string, planned: string, lateMin: number): string {
  const how =
    lateMin === 0
      ? 'on time'
      : `${formatDuration(Math.abs(lateMin))} ${lateMin > 0 ? 'later' : 'earlier'}`;
  return `Served at ${served}. Planned for ${planned}: ${how}.`;
}
export function cookStaleBody(title: string, weekday: string, time: string): string {
  return `You started cooking ${title} on ${weekday} at ${time}, more than 8 hours ago.`;
}
export function cookTooLate(time: string, day: string | null): string {
  const when = day === null ? time : `${time} ${day}`;
  return `This plan was for ${when}, too long ago to cook against. Choose a new serving time on the Plan screen.`;
}

export function cookAlertsText(mode: 'off' | 'on' | 'reload', canVibrate: boolean): string {
  const base =
    mode === 'off'
      ? 'Turn on to hear a chime and feel a buzz when a timer ends. The browser allows sound only after you tap here.'
      : mode === 'on'
        ? 'A chime and a buzz play when a timer ends. Silent mode may still mute them.'
        : 'Sound was on before the page reloaded. Tap once to turn it on again.';
  return canVibrate ? base : `${base} This device cannot vibrate.`;
}
export const cookStartReached = (titles: readonly string[]): string =>
  `Time to start: ${titles.join(', ')}.`;

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createWakeLock } from '../../../src/web/state/wake-lock.js';
import type { SentinelLike } from '../../../src/web/state/wake-lock.js';

/** A fake sentinel: records releases and lets the test fire the browser's `release` event. */
function fakeSentinel(): SentinelLike & { releases: number; fire(): void } {
  const listeners: (() => void)[] = [];
  return {
    releases: 0,
    release() {
      this.releases += 1;
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    addEventListener(_type, listener) {
      listeners.push(listener);
    },
    fire() {
      for (const listener of listeners) listener();
    },
  };
}

describe('createWakeLock (CM6.1, CM6.2)', () => {
  it('CM6.1 acquire requests a screen lock once and reports held; a second acquire makes no request', async () => {
    const sentinel = fakeSentinel();
    const requests: string[] = [];
    const port = createWakeLock({
      wakeLock: {
        request: (type) => {
          requests.push(type);
          return Promise.resolve(sentinel);
        },
      },
    });
    expect(port.status()).toBe('idle');
    expect(await port.acquire()).toBe('held');
    expect(await port.acquire()).toBe('held');
    expect(requests).toEqual(['screen']);
    expect(port.status()).toBe('held');
  });

  it('CM6.2 without navigator.wakeLock the status is unsupported', async () => {
    const port = createWakeLock({});
    expect(await port.acquire()).toBe('unsupported');
    expect(port.status()).toBe('unsupported');
  });

  it('CM6.2 a refused request is refused, not an error', async () => {
    const port = createWakeLock({ wakeLock: { request: () => Promise.reject(new Error('no')) } });
    expect(await port.acquire()).toBe('refused');
    expect(port.status()).toBe('refused');
  });

  it("CM6.1 the browser's release event makes the status idle, so the next acquire asks again", async () => {
    const first = fakeSentinel();
    const second = fakeSentinel();
    const queue = [first, second];
    let requests = 0;
    const port = createWakeLock({
      wakeLock: {
        request: () => {
          requests += 1;
          return Promise.resolve(queue.shift() ?? second);
        },
      },
    });
    await port.acquire();
    first.fire();
    expect(port.status()).toBe('idle');
    expect(await port.acquire()).toBe('held');
    expect(requests).toBe(2);
  });

  it('CM6.1 release calls the sentinel once and leaves the status idle; a second release does nothing', async () => {
    const sentinel = fakeSentinel();
    const port = createWakeLock({ wakeLock: { request: () => Promise.resolve(sentinel) } });
    await port.acquire();
    await port.release();
    await port.release();
    expect(sentinel.releases).toBe(1);
    expect(port.status()).toBe('idle');
  });
});
EOF

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createAlerts } from '../../../src/web/state/alerts.js';
import type { AudioContextLike } from '../../../src/web/state/alerts.js';

interface Call {
  what: string;
  args: unknown[];
}

/** A fake Web Audio context that records every call the port makes. */
function fakeAudio(): { Ctor: new () => AudioContextLike; calls: Call[]; made: () => number } {
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  let made = 0;
  class FakeContext implements AudioContextLike {
    currentTime = 10;
    destination = { name: 'destination' };
    constructor() {
      made += 1;
    }
    resume(): Promise<void> {
      calls.push({ what: 'resume', args: [] });
      return Promise.resolve();
    }
    createOscillator() {
      const osc = {
        type: '',
        frequency: { value: 0 },
        connect: (): void => {
          calls.push({ what: 'osc.connect', args: [] });
        },
        start: (t: number): void => {
          calls.push({ what: 'osc.start', args: [t, osc.frequency.value, osc.type] });
        },
        stop: (t: number): void => {
          calls.push({ what: 'osc.stop', args: [t] });
        },
      };
      return osc;
    }
    createGain() {
      return {
        gain: {
          value: 0,
          setValueAtTime: (v: number, t: number): void => {
            calls.push({ what: 'gain.set', args: [v, t] });
          },
          linearRampToValueAtTime: (v: number, t: number): void => {
            calls.push({ what: 'gain.ramp', args: [v, t] });
          },
        },
        connect: (): void => {
          calls.push({ what: 'gain.connect', args: [] });
        },
      };
    }
  }
  return { Ctor: FakeContext, calls, made: () => made };
}

describe('createAlerts (CM4.3, CM4.4)', () => {
  it('CM4.4 starts not ready and creates no context until enable', () => {
    const audio = fakeAudio();
    const port = createAlerts({ AudioContext: audio.Ctor, navigator: {} });
    expect(port.ready()).toBe(false);
    expect(audio.made()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('CM4.4 enable creates one context, resumes it, and is ready; a second enable reuses it', async () => {
    const audio = fakeAudio();
    const port = createAlerts({ AudioContext: audio.Ctor, navigator: {} });
    expect(await port.enable()).toBe(true);
    expect(await port.enable()).toBe(true);
    expect(audio.made()).toBe(1);
    expect(audio.calls.filter((c) => c.what === 'resume')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(port.ready()).toBe(true);
  });

  it('CM4.3 play sounds two 880 Hz sine beeps starting at 0 and 0.25 s, with 10 ms gain ramps, and vibrates 200-100-200', async () => {
    const audio = fakeAudio();
    const vibrations: number[][] = [];
    const port = createAlerts({
      AudioContext: audio.Ctor,
      navigator: {
        vibrate: (pattern: number[]) => {
          vibrations.push(pattern);
          return true;
        },
      },
    });
    await port.enable();
    audio.calls.length = 0;
    port.play();
    expect(audio.calls.filter((c) => c.what === 'osc.start').map((c) => c.args)).toEqual([
      [10, 880, 'sine'],
      [10.25, 880, 'sine'],
    ]);
    expect(audio.calls.filter((c) => c.what === 'osc.stop').map((c) => c.args)).toEqual([
      [10.15],
      [10.4],
    ]);
    // Each beep: silent at its start, up to 0.2 in 10 ms, back to silent over its last 10 ms.
    const gain = audio.calls
      .filter((c) => c.what.startsWith('gain.') && c.what !== 'gain.connect')
      .map((c) => [c.what, ...c.args]);
    expect(gain).toEqual([
      ['gain.set', 0, 10],
      ['gain.ramp', 0.2, 10.01],
      ['gain.set', 0.2, 10.14],
      ['gain.ramp', 0, 10.15],
      ['gain.set', 0, 10.25],
      ['gain.ramp', 0.2, 10.26],
      ['gain.set', 0.2, 10.39],
      ['gain.ramp', 0, 10.4],
    ]);
    expect(vibrations).toEqual([[200, 100, 200]]);
  });

  it('CM4.4 without AudioContext enable gives false and play is silent', async () => {
    const vibrations: number[][] = [];
    const port = createAlerts({
      navigator: {
        vibrate: (p: number[]) => {
          vibrations.push(p);
          return true;
        },
      },
    });
    expect(await port.enable()).toBe(false);
    expect(port.ready()).toBe(false);
    port.play();
    expect(vibrations).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('CM4.3 a device without vibrate still plays the sound and does not throw', async () => {
    const audio = fakeAudio();
    const port = createAlerts({ AudioContext: audio.Ctor, navigator: {} });
    await port.enable();
    audio.calls.length = 0;
    expect(() => port.play()).not.toThrow();
    expect(audio.calls.filter((c) => c.what === 'osc.start')).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});

export type WakeLockStatus = 'idle' | 'held' | 'unsupported' | 'refused';
export interface SentinelLike {
  release(): Promise<void>;
  addEventListener(type: 'release', listener: () => void): void;
}
export interface WakeLockPort {
  acquire(): Promise<WakeLockStatus>;
  release(): Promise<void>;
  status(): WakeLockStatus;
}
export function createWakeLock(nav: {
  wakeLock?: { request(type: 'screen'): Promise<SentinelLike> };
}): WakeLockPort {
  void nav;
  return {
    acquire: () => Promise.resolve('idle'),
    release: () => Promise.resolve(),
    status: () => 'idle',
  };
}

export interface OscillatorLike {
  type: string;
  frequency: { value: number };
  connect(node: unknown): void;
  start(when: number): void;
  stop(when: number): void;
}
export interface GainLike {
  gain: {
    value: number;
    setValueAtTime(value: number, when: number): void;
    linearRampToValueAtTime(value: number, when: number): void;
  };
  connect(node: unknown): void;
}
export interface AudioContextLike {
  currentTime: number;
  destination: unknown;
  resume(): Promise<void>;
  createOscillator(): OscillatorLike;
  createGain(): GainLike;
}
export interface AlertPort {
  enable(): Promise<boolean>;
  ready(): boolean;
  play(): void;
}
export function createAlerts(win: {
  AudioContext?: new () => AudioContextLike;
  navigator: { vibrate?: (pattern: number[]) => boolean };
}): AlertPort {
  void win;
  return { enable: () => Promise.resolve(false), ready: () => false, play: () => undefined };
}

[tool:execute FAILED] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/state/wake-lock.ts <<'EOF'
/** The screen wake lock behind a small port, so it can be faked (design, "Ports and effects"). */
export type WakeLockStatus = 'idle' | 'held' | 'unsupported' | 'refused';
export interface SentinelLike {
  release(): Promise<void>;
  addEventListener(type: 'release', listener: () => void): void;
}
export interface WakeLockPort {
  acquire(): Promise<WakeLockStatus>;
  release(): Promise<void>;
  status(): WakeLockStatus;
}

export function createWakeLock(nav: {
  wakeLock?: { request(type: 'screen'): Promise<SentinelLike> };
}): WakeLockPort {
  let status: WakeLockStatus = 'idle';
  let sentinel: SentinelLike | null = null;
  return {
    async acquire() {
      if (status === 'held') return status;
      if (nav.wakeLock === undefined) {
        status = 'unsupported';
        return status;
      }
      try {
        const granted = await nav.wakeLock.request('screen');
        sentinel = granted;
        status = 'held';
        granted.addEventListener('release', () => {
          if (sentinel === granted) {
            sentinel = null;
            status = 'idle';
          }
        });
      } catch {
        status = 'refused';
      }
      return status;
    },
    async release() {
      const held = sentinel;
      sentinel = null;
      if (status === 'held') status = 'idle';
      if (held !== null) await held.release();
    },
    status: () => status,
  };
}
EOF
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/web/state/alerts.ts'
s=open(p).read()
i=s.index("export function createAlerts")
s=s[:i]+'''const BEEP_HZ = 880;
const BEEP_S = 0.15;
const BEEP_STEP_S = 0.25;
const RAMP_S = 0.01;
const BEEP_GAIN = 0.2;
const VIBRATE_PATTERN = [200, 100, 200];

/** The chime and the buzz of a timer ending. `enable` must run inside a tap (CM4.4). */
export function createAlerts(win: {
  AudioContext?: new () => AudioContextLike;
  navigator: { vibrate?: (pattern: number[]) => boolean };
}): AlertPort {
  let context: AudioContextLike | null = null;
  return {
    async enable() {
      if (win.AudioContext === undefined) return false;
      context ??= new win.AudioContext();
      try {
        await context.resume();
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    },
    ready: () => context !== null,
    play() {
      if (context === null) return;
      const ctx = context;
      for (const offset of [0, BEEP_STEP_S]) {
        const start = ctx.currentTime + offset;
        const end = start + BEEP_S;
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'sine';
        osc.frequency.value = BEEP_HZ;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, start);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(BEEP_GAIN, start + RAMP_S);
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(BEEP_GAIN, end - RAMP_S);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, end);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);
        osc.start(start);
        osc.stop(end);
      }
      win.navigator.vibrate?.(VIBRATE_PATTERN);
    },
  };
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)

describe('createAlerts""")
s=s.replace("filter((c) => c.what === 'osc.start').map((c) => c.args))","filter((c) => c.what === 'osc.start').map((c) => c.args.map(ms)))")
s=s.replace("filter((c) => c.what === 'osc.stop').map((c) => c.args))","filter((c) => c.what === 'osc.stop').map((c) => c.args.map(ms)))")
s=s.replace(".map((c) => [c.what, ...c.args]);",".map((c) => [c.what, ...c.args.map(ms)]);")
open(p,'w').write(s)

     A build system stitched onto a kitchen. Paste the recipes for several dishes and get one
     schedule in which every dish lands hot at the same time.
     
    -Work in progress for Kiroween 2026 (category: Frankenstein). The plan is in
    -[`docs/BRIEF.md`](docs/BRIEF.md); how Kiro is being used is in [`.kiro/`](.kiro) and
    -[`docs/kiro-evidence/`](docs/kiro-evidence).
    +Built for Kiroween 2026, category **Frankenstein**. MIT licence.
    +
    +## The problem
    +
    +A recipe is written as if you had the whole stove, the whole oven and both hands to yourself.
    +Cook four at once and you have a scheduling problem that you solve in your head while something
    +burns. Build systems solved that problem decades ago: targets, prerequisites, a limit on
    +parallel jobs, incremental rebuilds when one input changes.

    import { useDispatch } from '../state/hooks.js';

    /** Start time, serving time (with the day when not today) and span (R5.4). */
    export function PlanSummary(props: { view: ScheduleView; chosenMs: number; nowMs: number }): VNode {
      const { view, chosenMs, nowMs } = props;
      const dispatch = useDispatch();
      const servingMs = clockAt(chosenMs, view.servingMin);
      const startMs = clockAt(chosenMs, view.startMin);
      const earliestMs = earliestServing(nowMs, view.spanMin);
      return (
        <div class="summary">
          <p class="summary__start">{startAt(formatClock(startMs))}</p>
          <p>{serveAt(formatClock(servingMs), formatDay(servingMs, nowMs))}</p>
          <p>{takes(formatDuration(view.spanMin))}</p>
          {startMs < nowMs && (
            <div class="summary__past no-print">
              <p>{pastStart(formatDuration(view.spanMin), formatClock(earliestMs))}</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                class="button button--primary"
                onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'serving/set-instant', atMs: earliestMs })}
              >
                {serveAtButton(formatClock(earliestMs))}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      );
    }

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='e2e/support.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s+='''
/**
 * Installs the fake clock and pauses it at `iso` (a UTC instant on the fixed day; London is on
 * GMT then): time moves only through `page.clock.runFor` and `fastForward` (cooking-mode e2e).
 */
export async function pauseClockAt(page: Page, iso: string): Promise<void> {
  const at = new Date(iso);
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(at.getTime() - 60_000) });
  await page.clock.pauseAt(at);
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
cat > e2e/cook-start.spec.ts <<'EOF'
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST } from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import { pauseClockAt, seedDinner } from './support.js';

test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });

// The plan is Sunday roast for 19:30 on Wed 2026-11-11 (London is on GMT): the first step
// starts at 17:40 (cooking-mode design, CM-a and CM-b).
test.describe('starting to cook (CM1.1 to CM1.4)', () => {
  test('CM1.2 at 17:30 "Start cooking" counts down 10:00 to 17:40 and offers "Start now"', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await pauseClockAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:30:00Z');
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Start cooking' }).click();

    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Right now' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: 'Cooking starts at 17:40' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('timer', { name: 'Time until cooking starts' })).toHaveText('10:00');
    await expect(page.getByText('Start now to serve at 19:20, 10 min earlier.')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('First: Season and truss the chicken (Roast chicken)')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('Serve at 19:30')).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('CM4.1 a minute later the countdown reads 09:00', async ({ page }) => {
    await pauseClockAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:30:00Z');
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Start cooking' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole('timer')).toHaveText('10:00');
    await page.clock.runFor(60_000);
    await expect(page.getByRole('timer')).toHaveText('09:00');
  });

  test('CM1.3 "Start now" moves serving to 19:20 and ends the countdown', async ({ page }) => {
    await pauseClockAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:30:00Z');
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Start cooking' }).click();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start now' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText('Serve at 19:20')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('Planned for 19:30')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('timer')).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test('CM1.4 a session begun at 17:52 rebuilds at once: serve at 19:42', async ({ page }) => {
    await pauseClockAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:52:00Z');
    await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, '19:30');
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Start cooking' }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText('Serve at 19:42')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('Planned for 19:30')).toBeVisible();
  });
});
EOF

cat > src/web/state/cook-hooks.ts <<'EOF'
import { createContext, createElement } from 'preact';
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useContext, useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { AlertPort } from './alerts.js';
import { systemClock } from './clock.js';
import { TICK_MS } from './cook-session.js';
import { useDispatch } from './hooks.js';
import type { WakeLockPort } from './wake-lock.js';

/** The device ports of the Cook screen, provided once by `main.tsx` (and faked in tests). */
export interface CookPorts {
  wakeLock: WakeLockPort;
  alerts: AlertPort;
}

const PortsContext = createContext<CookPorts | null>(null);

export function CookPortsProvider(props: {
  ports: CookPorts;
  children: ComponentChildren;
}): ComponentChildren {
  return createElement(PortsContext.Provider, { value: props.ports }, props.children);
}

export function useCookPorts(): CookPorts {
  const ports = useContext(PortsContext);
  if (ports === null) throw new Error('useCookPorts needs a CookPortsProvider');
  return ports;
}

/**
 * Effects while the Cook screen is mounted. Now: a `cook/tick` every `TICK_MS` and when the
 * page becomes visible. A tick changes nothing in the state; it lets the screen catch up.
 */
export function useCookEffects(): void {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  useEffect(() => {
    const tick = (): void => dispatch({ type: 'cook/tick', nowMs: systemClock.nowMs() });
    const id = setInterval(tick, TICK_MS);
    const onVisible = (): void => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') tick();
    };
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(id);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
    };
  }, [dispatch]);
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/Countdown.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { cookCountdown } from '../copy.js';

/**
 * A big mono countdown (ui.md: 56 px, tabular). `role="timer"` is not live, so a screen reader
 * does not read every second (CM9.4). Over zero it reads `+02:10`.
 */
export function Countdown(props: { remainingMs: number; label: string }): VNode {
  return (
    <div class="countdown mono" role="timer" aria-live="off" aria-label={props.label}>
      {cookCountdown(props.remainingMs)}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/CookHeader.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { cookPlannedFor, serveAt } from '../copy.js';
import { formatClock, formatDay } from '../state/clock.js';

/** The clock, the serving time, and the planned one when the session moved it (CM2.1). */
export function CookHeader(props: {
  nowMs: number;
  servingMs: number;
  plannedServingMs: number;
}): VNode {
  const { nowMs, servingMs, plannedServingMs } = props;
  return (
    <div class="cook-header">
      <p class="cook-header__clock mono">{formatClock(nowMs)}</p>
      <p class="cook-header__serving">
        <span class="mono">{serveAt(formatClock(servingMs), formatDay(servingMs, nowMs))}</span>
        {servingMs !== plannedServingMs && (
          <span class="cook-header__planned muted mono">
            {' '}
            {cookPlannedFor(formatClock(plannedServingMs))}
          </span>
        )}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/CookCountdown.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY, cookFirst, cookStartNow, cookStartsAt } from '../copy.js';
import { formatClock, MINUTE_MS } from '../state/clock.js';
import type { CookView } from '../state/cook-view.js';
import { Countdown } from './Countdown.js';

/** Before the first step: the time to the start, the first steps, and "Start now" (CM1.2, CM1.3). */
export function CookCountdown(props: {
  view: CookView;
  shiftMin: number;
  onStartNow: () => void;
}): VNode {
  const { view, shiftMin } = props;
  const first = view.upNext
    .filter((item) => item.startMs === view.startMs)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((item) => ({ title: item.title, dish: item.dishName }));
  const newServing = formatClock(view.servingMs - shiftMin * MINUTE_MS);
  return (
    <section class="cook-countdown" aria-labelledby="cook-countdown-heading">
      <h2 id="cook-countdown-heading">{cookStartsAt(formatClock(view.startMs))}</h2>
      <Countdown remainingMs={view.startMs - view.nowMs} label={COOK_COPY.countdownLabel} />
      {first.length > 0 && <p>{cookFirst(first)}</p>}
      <p>{cookStartNow(newServing, shiftMin)}</p>
      <button type="button" class="button button--primary button--cook" onClick={props.onStartNow}>
        {COOK_COPY.startNow}
      </button>
    </section>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/screens/Cook.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';
import { CookCountdown } from '../components/CookCountdown.js';
import { CookHeader } from '../components/CookHeader.js';
import { COOK_COPY, cookTooLate } from '../copy.js';
import { formatClock, formatDay, systemClock } from '../state/clock.js';
import { canBegin, startNowShiftMin } from '../state/cook-session.js';
import { useCookEffects } from '../state/cook-hooks.js';
import { cookView } from '../state/cook-view.js';
import { useAppState, useDispatch, useNow } from '../state/hooks.js';
import { hashOf } from '../state/route.js';
import { selectSchedule } from '../state/store.js';

/** "Right now": begins a session from the Plan screen's schedule, then shows it (CM1, CM2). */
export function Cook(): VNode {
  const cook = useAppState((state) => state.cook);
  const serving = useAppState((state) => state.dinner?.serving ?? null);
  const scheduled = useAppState(selectSchedule);
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const nowMs = useNow(systemClock, 1000);
  useCookEffects();

  const schedule = scheduled !== null && scheduled.ok ? scheduled.value : null;
  const begin =
    cook === null && schedule !== null && serving !== null
      ? canBegin(schedule, serving.atMs, nowMs)
      : null;

  // CM1.1: the first visit begins the session against the Plan screen's schedule.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (cook !== null || schedule === null || serving === null) return;
    dispatch({ type: 'cook/begin', schedule, axisMs: serving.atMs, nowMs: systemClock.nowMs() });
  }, [cook === null]);

  const tooLate = begin !== null && !begin.ok && begin.error.kind === 'too_late';
  if (cook === null) {
    return (
      <main class="screen cook">
        <h1 tabIndex={-1}>{COOK_COPY.h1}</h1>
        {tooLate && serving !== null && (
          <div class="notice-block">
            <p>{cookTooLate(formatClock(serving.atMs), formatDay(serving.atMs, nowMs))}</p>
            <a class="button" href={hashOf('plan')}>
              {COOK_COPY.goToPlan}
            </a>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>
    );
  }

  const view = cookView(cook, nowMs);
  return (
    <main class="screen cook">
      <h1 tabIndex={-1}>{COOK_COPY.h1}</h1>
      <CookHeader
        nowMs={nowMs}
        servingMs={view.servingMs}
        plannedServingMs={view.plannedServingMs}
      />
      {view.phase === 'countdown' && (
        <CookCountdown
          view={view}
          shiftMin={startNowShiftMin(cook.core, nowMs)}
          onStartNow={() => dispatch({ type: 'cook/start-now', nowMs: systemClock.nowMs() })}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

    /** "Start now": the minutes until the first start, rounded up so the start is reached. */
    export function startNowShiftMin(core: CookCore, nowMs: number): number {
      const startMs = clockAt(core.axisMs, earliestStartMin(core.schedule));
      return Math.max(1, Math.ceil((startMs - nowMs) / MINUTE_MS));
    }
    
    export type Phase = 'countdown' | 'cooking' | 'finished' | 'stale';
    
    /** Ids of steps no other step lists in `after`. */

    /**
     * Cooking-mode reducer for plan-ui's `reduce` (R9.9). An action that changes nothing returns
     * `state` itself; `cook/begin` with a session already there, or one that cannot begin, too.
     */
    export function reduceCook<S extends { cook: CookState | null }>(state: S, action: CookAction): S {
      if (action.type === 'cook/stop') {
        return state.cook === null ? state : { ...state, cook: null };
      }
      if (action.type === 'cook/begin') {
        if (state.cook !== null) return state;
        const begun = beginSession(action.schedule, action.axisMs, action.nowMs);
        return begun.ok ? { ...state, cook: begun.value } : state;
      }
      if (state.cook === null) return state;
      const next = applyCook(state.cook, action);
      return next === state.cook ? state : { ...state, cook: next };
    }
    exit 0

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='e2e/support.ts'
s=open(p).read()
i=s.index("/**\n * Installs the fake clock")
s=s[:i]+'''/**
 * Fixes `Date.now()` at `iso` (a UTC instant on the fixed day; London is on GMT then) for the
 * cooking-mode flows. Timers keep running: a paused clock (`install` + `pauseAt`) also freezes
 * `requestAnimationFrame`, which Preact uses to run `useEffect`, so the screen would never
 * update after a tap. The app re-reads the time at most a second after it moves.
 */
export async function fixClockAt(page: Page, iso: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.clock.setFixedTime(new Date(iso));
}

/** Moves the fixed time forward by `ms`; the screen catches up at its next one-second read. */
export async function advanceClock(page: Page, ms: number): Promise<void> {
  const now = await page.evaluate(() => Date.now());
  await page.clock.setFixedTime(new Date(now + ms));
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)
p='e2e/cook-start.spec.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("pauseClockAt","fixClockAt").replace("await page.clock.runFor(60_000);","await advanceClock(page, 60_000);").replace("import { fixClockAt, seedDinner }","import { advanceClock, fixClockAt, seedDinner }")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

cat > e2e/cook-roast.spec.ts <<'EOF'
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { HOME_KITCHEN, SUNDAY_ROAST, TWO_COOK_KITCHEN } from '../test/support/fixtures.js';
import type { Kitchen } from '../src/core/index.js';
import { advanceClock, fixClockAt, seedDinner } from './support.js';

test.use({ locale: 'en-GB', timezoneId: 'Europe/London' });

/** Sunday roast for 19:30 on Wed 2026-11-11, "Start cooking" tapped at `iso`. */
async function cookAt(
  page: Page,
  iso: string,
  kitchen: Kitchen = HOME_KITCHEN,
  serve: '19:30' = '19:30',
): Promise<void> {
  await fixClockAt(page, iso);
  await seedDinner(page, SUNDAY_ROAST, kitchen, serve);
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Start cooking' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Right now' })).toBeVisible();
}

const dock = (page: Page) => page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Actions' });

test.describe('Do now and the dock (CM2.2 to CM2.4, CM3.1, CM3.5, CM3.6, CM9.3)', () => {
  test('CM3.1 at 17:40 the dock names the prep step with Start; Start moves focus to Done', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await cookAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:40:00Z');
    await expect(dock(page).getByText('Season and truss the chicken')).toBeVisible();
    await dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Start', exact: true }).click();
    const done = dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true });
    await expect(done).toBeVisible();
    await expect(done).toBeFocused();
  });

  test('CM2.4 at 17:50 Done on the prep step shows the roast as "Start, then leave it" and the shred in the dock', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await cookAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:40:00Z');
    await dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Start', exact: true }).click();
    await advanceClock(page, 10 * 60_000);
    // The screen reads the time once a second; wait for the 17:50 card before tapping.
    await expect(dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true }).click();

    const roast = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Roast the chicken' });
    await expect(roast.getByText('Start, then leave it')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(roast.getByRole('button', { name: /^Start/ })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(dock(page).getByText('Shred the greens')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Start', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('CM2.2 with two cooks at 17:40 there are a Cook 1 and a Cook 2 card', async ({ page }) => {
    await cookAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:40:00Z', TWO_COOK_KITCHEN);
    await expect(page.getByText('Cook 1', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('Cook 2', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 3, name: 'Season and truss the chicken' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 3, name: 'Shred the greens' })).toBeVisible();
  });
});
EOF

cat > src/web/components/StepCardBody.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY, cookDue, cookReady, cookWaitsFor } from '../copy.js';
import { formatClock, MINUTE_MS } from '../state/clock.js';
import type { CardStep } from '../state/cook-view.js';
import { Countdown } from './Countdown.js';

/** The id of the primary button of a step: `focusAfter` points at it (CM9.3). */
export function actionId(stepId: string, action: 'start' | 'done'): string {
  return `act-${stepId}-${action}`;
}

/** The words of a card's second line: when it is due, or when a running step is ready. */
function metaOf(card: CardStep, nowMs: number): string {
  if (card.state === 'running' && card.endsAtMs !== null) {
    return cookReady(card.dishName, formatClock(card.endsAtMs));
  }
  const since = nowMs - card.plannedStartMs >= MINUTE_MS;
  return cookDue(card.dishName, card.minutes, formatClock(card.plannedStartMs), since);
}

/**
 * What every step card shows: title (large), detail, dish, duration and time, the prerequisites
 * not done, and the countdown of a running step (CM2.3). The action button is the caller's.
 */
export function StepCardBody(props: { card: CardStep; nowMs: number }): VNode {
  const { card, nowMs } = props;
  return (
    <>
      <h3 id={`card-${card.stepId}`} tabIndex={-1} class="cook-card__title">
        {card.title}
      </h3>
      {card.detail !== null && <p class="cook-card__detail">{card.detail}</p>}
      <p class="cook-card__meta muted mono">{metaOf(card, nowMs)}</p>
      {card.waitsFor.length > 0 && (
        <p class="cook-card__warn">
          <span aria-hidden="true">!</span> {cookWaitsFor(card.waitsFor)}
        </p>
      )}
      {card.remainingMs !== null && (
        <Countdown remainingMs={card.remainingMs} label={COOK_COPY.timeLeft} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

/** The Start or Done button of a card. Its name carries the step title, which the dock shows beside it. */
export function StepButton(props: {
  card: CardStep;
  onStart: (stepId: string) => void;
  onDone: (stepId: string) => void;
}): VNode {
  const { card } = props;
  const action = card.state === 'due' ? 'start' : 'done';
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      id={actionId(card.stepId, action)}
      class="button button--primary button--cook"
      onClick={() => (action === 'start' ? props.onStart(card.stepId) : props.onDone(card.stepId))}
    >
      {action === 'start' ? COOK_COPY.start : COOK_COPY.done}
      <span class="visually-hidden"> {card.title}</span>
    </button>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/LaneCard.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY, cookNext, laneName } from '../copy.js';
import { formatClock, MINUTE_MS } from '../state/clock.js';
import type { LaneCard as LaneCardData } from '../state/cook-view.js';
import { StepButton, StepCardBody } from './StepCardBody.js';

interface Props {
  lane: LaneCardData;
  /** The label "Cook 2" is shown only when the kitchen has several cooks. */
  labelled: boolean;
  nowMs: number;
  /** The step whose button is in the dock, so its card has none. */
  dockStepId: string | null;
  onStart: (stepId: string) => void;
  onDone: (stepId: string) => void;
}

/** One cook lane: what that cook is doing or should do next, and any further due steps (CM2.2). */
export function LaneCard(props: Props): VNode {
  const { lane, nowMs } = props;
  const label = props.labelled ? laneName('cook', lane.cookLane) : null;
  const { current } = lane;
  return (
    <section
      class="cook-card"
      aria-labelledby={current === null ? `card-lane-${lane.cookLane}` : `card-${current.stepId}`}
    >
      {label !== null && <p class="cook-card__label mono">{label}</p>}
      {current === null ? (
        <>
          <h3 id={`card-lane-${lane.cookLane}`} tabIndex={-1} class="cook-card__title">
            {COOK_COPY.nothingToDo}
          </h3>
          <p class="cook-card__meta muted">
            {lane.next === null
              ? COOK_COPY.nothingMore
              : cookNext(
                  lane.next.title,
                  formatClock(lane.next.startMs),
                  Math.ceil((lane.next.startMs - nowMs) / MINUTE_MS),
                )}
          </p>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <StepCardBody card={current} nowMs={nowMs} />
          {current.stepId !== props.dockStepId && (
            <StepButton card={current} onStart={props.onStart} onDone={props.onDone} />
          )}
        </>
      )}
      {lane.also.length > 0 && (
        <div class="cook-card__also">
          <h4 class="cook-card__also-heading">{COOK_COPY.alsoDue}</h4>
          {lane.also.map((card) => (
            <div class="cook-card__also-item" key={card.stepId}>
              <StepCardBody card={card} nowMs={nowMs} />
              <StepButton card={card} onStart={props.onStart} onDone={props.onDone} />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import type { CardStep } from '../state/cook-view.js';
import { StepButton, StepCardBody } from './StepCardBody.js';

/** A due hands-free step: start it, then leave it (CM2.4). Dashed border: hands-free (ui.md). */
export function StartCard(props: {
  card: CardStep;
  nowMs: number;
  dockStepId: string | null;
  onStart: (stepId: string) => void;
  onDone: (stepId: string) => void;
}): VNode {
  const { card } = props;
  return (

cat > src/web/components/ActionDock.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import type { CardStep, CookView } from '../state/cook-view.js';
import { actionId } from './StepCardBody.js';

/** The card the dock's primary acts on: lane 1's current step, else an overrun hands-free step. */
export function dockCard(view: CookView): CardStep | null {
  if (view.dock === null) return null;
  const id = view.dock.stepId;
  const all = [...view.lanes.flatMap((lane) => [lane.current, ...lane.also]), ...view.running];
  return all.find((card) => card?.stepId === id) ?? null;
}

/**
 * The thumb zone: sticky at the bottom of the screen, full-width primary actions (ui.md, CM3.6).
 * The dock's step is not also given a button in its card.
 */
export function ActionDock(props: {
  card: CardStep | null;
  onStart: (stepId: string) => void;
  onDone: (stepId: string) => void;
}): VNode {
  const { card } = props;
  const action = card === null ? null : card.state === 'due' ? 'start' : 'done';
  return (
    <div class="action-dock" role="region" aria-label={COOK_COPY.dockLabel}>
      {card !== null && action !== null && (
        <>
          <p class="action-dock__title">{card.title}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            id={actionId(card.stepId, action)}
            class="button button--primary button--cook"
            onClick={() => (action === 'start' ? props.onStart(card.stepId) : props.onDone(card.stepId))}
          >
            {action === 'start' ? COOK_COPY.start : COOK_COPY.done}
          </button>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat >> e2e/cook-roast.spec.ts <<'EOF'

/** CM-c up to the shred: prep done, the roast and the shred started 17:50, the shred done 17:55. */
async function tapToCmF(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const click = (name: string) => dock(page).getByRole('button', { name, exact: true }).click();
  await click('Start');
  await advanceClock(page, 10 * 60_000);
  await expect(dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await click('Done'); // prep, 17:50
  const roast = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Roast the chicken' });
  await roast.getByRole('button', { name: /^Start/ }).click(); // roast, 17:50
  await click('Start'); // shred, 17:50
  await advanceClock(page, 5 * 60_000);
  await expect(dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await click('Done'); // shred, 17:55
}

test.describe('Running and Needs more time (CM2.5, CM3.1, CM4.1, CM4.2)', () => {
  test('CM2.5 the roast started at 17:50 reads ready at 19:05 with 75:00; at 19:05 its time is up and the dock says Done', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await cookAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:40:00Z');
    await dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Start', exact: true }).click();
    await advanceClock(page, 10 * 60_000);
    await expect(dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true }).click();
    await page
      .getByRole('region', { name: 'Roast the chicken' })
      .getByRole('button', { name: /^Start/ })
      .click();

    const running = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Roast the chicken' });
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: 'Running' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(running.getByText('Roast chicken · ready at 19:05')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(running.getByRole('timer', { name: 'Time left' })).toHaveText('75:00');
  });

  test('CM4.2 CM3.1 with the shred done, at 19:05 the roast is "! Time is up", the dock offers Done, and +5 min sits under Needs more time', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await cookAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:40:00Z');
    await tapToCmF(page);
    const running = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Roast the chicken' });
    await expect(running.getByRole('timer', { name: 'Time left' })).toHaveText('65:00');
    const group = running.getByRole('group', { name: 'Needs more time' });
    await expect(group.getByRole('button', { name: '+5 min' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(group.getByRole('button', { name: '+10 min' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(group.getByRole('button', { name: '+15 min' })).toBeVisible();

    await advanceClock(page, 70 * 60_000); // 17:55 -> 19:05
    await expect(running.getByText('! Time is up')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(dock(page).getByText('Roast the chicken')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(dock(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  });
});

import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY, cookExtra } from '../copy.js';
import { EXTRA_CHOICES } from '../state/cook-session.js';

/** "Needs more time": +5, +10 and +15 minutes on a running step (CM3.1). */
export function ExtraTime(props: {
  stepId: string;
  title: string;
  onExtra: (stepId: string, minutes: 5 | 10 | 15) => void;
}): VNode {
  return (
    <fieldset class="extra-time">
      <legend>{COOK_COPY.needsMoreTime}</legend>
      <div class="extra-time__buttons">
        {EXTRA_CHOICES.map((minutes) => (
          <button
            key={minutes}
            type="button"
            class="button button--cook"
            onClick={() => props.onExtra(props.stepId, minutes)}
          >
            {cookExtra(minutes)}
            <span class="visually-hidden"> for {props.title}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </fieldset>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/RunningCard.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY } from '../copy.js';
import type { CardStep } from '../state/cook-view.js';
import { ExtraTime } from './ExtraTime.js';
import { StepButton, StepCardBody } from './StepCardBody.js';

/** A hands-free step in progress: its countdown, "Time is up" once over, and more time (CM2.5). */
export function RunningCard(props: {
  card: CardStep;
  nowMs: number;
  dockStepId: string | null;
  onStart: (stepId: string) => void;
  onDone: (stepId: string) => void;
  onExtra: (stepId: string, minutes: 5 | 10 | 15) => void;
}): VNode {
  const { card } = props;
  const over = card.remainingMs !== null && card.remainingMs <= 0;
  return (
    <section class="cook-card cook-card--hands-free" aria-labelledby={`card-${card.stepId}`}>
      <StepCardBody card={card} nowMs={props.nowMs} />
      {over && (
        <p class="cook-card__warn">
          <span aria-hidden="true">!</span> {COOK_COPY.timeIsUp}
        </p>
      )}
      <ExtraTime stepId={card.stepId} title={card.title} onExtra={props.onExtra} />
      {card.stepId !== props.dockStepId && (
        <StepButton card={card} onStart={props.onStart} onDone={props.onDone} />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

/* Needs more time */

import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY, cookIn } from '../copy.js';
import { formatClock } from '../state/clock.js';
import type { UpNextItem } from '../state/cook-view.js';

/** The next three steps that are not due yet, each with its wait and clock time (CM2.6). */
export function UpNext(props: { items: UpNextItem[] }): VNode {
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="up-next">
      <h2 id="up-next" tabIndex={-1}>
        {COOK_COPY.upNext}
      </h2>
      {props.items.length === 0 ? (
        <p>{COOK_COPY.nothingElse}</p>
      ) : (
        <ol class="up-next">
          {props.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.stepId} class="up-next__item">
              <span class="up-next__title">{item.title}</span>
              <span class="up-next__meta muted">
                {item.dishName} · {cookIn(item.inMin)} ·{' '}
                <time class="mono" dateTime={new Date(item.startMs).toISOString()}>
                  {formatClock(item.startMs)}
                </time>
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

cat > src/web/components/DishStrip.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { COOK_COPY, cookDishChip, cookDishState } from '../copy.js';
import { formatClock } from '../state/clock.js';
import type { DishState } from '../state/cook-view.js';

function stateWords(state: DishState['state']): string {
  return state.kind === 'next'
    ? cookDishState('next', formatClock(state.startMs))
    : cookDishState(state.kind, '');
}

/** One chip per dish: steps done out of its steps and a state word; never colour alone (CM2.7). */
export function DishStrip(props: { dishes: DishState[] }): VNode {
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="dishes">
      <h2 id="dishes" tabIndex={-1}>
        {COOK_COPY.dishes}
      </h2>
      <ul class="dish-strip" aria-label={COOK_COPY.dishes}>
        {props.dishes.map((dish) => (
          <li key={dish.dishId} class="dish-strip__chip">
            <span class="dish-strip__count">{cookDishChip(dish.name, dish.done, dish.total)}</span>
            <span class="dish-strip__state">
              {dish.state.kind === 'done' && <span aria-hidden="true">✓ </span>}
              {stateWords(dish.state)}
            </span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}
EOF

test.describe('the build log (CM5.1, CM5.3 to CM5.6)', () => {
  test('CM5.3 CM-c: +15 min on the roast at 18:30 logs RB-a: lines, serving, warning, up-to-date list, announcement', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await cookAt(page, '2026-11-11T17:40:00Z');
    await tapToCmF(page);
    const log = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Build log' });
    await expect(log.getByText('No rebuilds yet.')).toBeVisible();

    await advanceClock(page, 35 * 60_000); // 18:30
    const roast = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Roast the chicken' });
    await expect(roast.getByRole('timer')).toHaveText('35:00');
    await roast.getByRole('group', { name: 'Needs more time' }).getByRole('button', { name: '+15 min' }).click();

    await expect(log.locator('.log-line')).toHaveText([
      '18:30 rebuild: chicken.roast needs 15 more min',
      'chicken.rest 19:05 -> 19:20',
      'gravy.roux 19:10 -> 19:26',
      'gravy.simmer 19:12 -> 19:28',
      'greens.boil 19:20 -> 19:33',
      'greens.toss 19:24 -> 19:37',
      'gravy.strain 19:22 -> 19:38',
      'chicken.carve 19:25 -> 19:40',
      '3 steps up to date',
      'serve 19:30 -> 19:45',
    ]);
    await expect(page.getByText('Serve at 19:45')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('Planned for 19:30')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(log.getByText('Warning')).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(
      log.getByText(
        'Spring greens: \u201cToss the greens with butter\u201d will be ready 7 min before serving and can wait only 5 min. More ovens, burners or cooks may help.',
      ),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('.live-notice')).toHaveText(
      'Plan rebuilt: 7 steps moved. Serving is now 19:45. 1 new warning.',
    );

    await expect(log.locator('.log-line', { hasText: 'is up to date' })).toHaveCount(0);
    const toggle = log.getByRole('button', { name: 'Show the 3 steps that are up to date' });
    await expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    await toggle.click();
    await expect(log.locator('.log-line', { hasText: 'is up to date' })).toHaveText([
      'chicken.prep is up to date',
      'chicken.roast is up to date',
      'greens.shred is up to date',
    ]);
    await expect(
      log.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hide the steps that are up to date' }),
    ).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > src/web/components/LogEntryView.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { compareIds } from '../../core/index.js';
import {
  cookHideUpToDate,
  cookLogLines,
  cookShowUpToDate,
  holdWarning,
} from '../copy.js';
import type { LogLine } from '../copy.js';
import type { CookCore, LogEntry } from '../state/cook-slot.js';

/** A mono line to look at, and the sentence a screen reader gets instead (design, C3). */
function Line(props: { line: LogLine; class?: string }): VNode {
  return (
    <li class={props.class}>
      <span class="log-line mono" aria-hidden="true">
        {props.line.mono}
      </span>
      <span class="visually-hidden log-sentence">{props.line.sentence}</span>
    </li>
  );
}

/** The title of the step that uses `stepId`'s result first in the current schedule, or null. */
function firstUserTitle(core: CookCore, stepId: string): string | null {
  const start = new Map(core.schedule.steps.map((row) => [row.id, row.startMin]));
  const users = core.schedule.plan.steps
    .filter((step) => step.after.includes(stepId))
    .sort((a, b) => (start.get(a.id) ?? 0) - (start.get(b.id) ?? 0) || compareIds(a.id, b.id));
  return users[0]?.title ?? null;
}

/** One rebuild: its cause, moves, counts, serving, new warnings and the up-to-date list (CM5.3 to CM5.5). */
export function LogEntryView(props: { entry: LogEntry; core: CookCore }): VNode {
  const { entry, core } = props;
  const [listed, setListed] = useState(false);
  const steps = new Map(core.schedule.plan.steps.map((step) => [step.id, step]));
  const dishes = new Map(core.schedule.plan.dishes.map((dish) => [dish.id, dish.name]));
  const lines = cookLogLines(entry, {
    axisMs: core.axisMs,
    titleOf: (id) => steps.get(id)?.title ?? id,
  });
  const warnings = entry.result.ok ? entry.result.newWarnings : [];
  return (
    <li class="log-entry">
      <ul class="log-entry__lines">
        <Line line={lines.cause} class="log-entry__cause" />
        {lines.failed !== null && <Line line={lines.failed} />}
        {lines.moves.map((line, index) => (
          <Line key={index} line={line} />
        ))}
        {lines.unchanged !== null && <Line line={lines.unchanged} />}
        {lines.serving !== null && <Line line={lines.serving} />}
        {lines.nothing !== null && <Line line={lines.nothing} />}
      </ul>
      {warnings.map((warning) => {
        const step = steps.get(warning.stepId);
        if (step === undefined) return null;
        return (
          <p class="log-entry__warning" key={warning.stepId}>
            <span class="mark mark--warn">! Warning</span>{' '}
            {holdWarning(
              dishes.get(step.dish) ?? step.dish,
              step.title,
              warning.waitMin,
              warning.holdMin,
              firstUserTitle(core, warning.stepId),
            )}
          </p>
        );
      })}
      {lines.upToDate.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button log-entry__toggle"
            aria-expanded={listed}
            onClick={() => setListed(!listed)}
          >
            {listed ? cookHideUpToDate() : cookShowUpToDate(lines.upToDate.length)}
          </button>
          {listed && (
            <ul class="log-entry__lines">
              {lines.upToDate.map((line, index) => (
                <Line key={index} line={line} />
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </li>
  );
}
EOF
cat > src/web/components/BuildLog.tsx <<'EOF'
import type { VNode } from 'preact';
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { COOK_COPY, cookEarlier } from '../copy.js';
import type { CookCore } from '../state/cook-slot.js';
import { LogEntryView } from './LogEntryView.js';

/** The rebuilds of this session, newest first; older ones one tap away (CM5.3, CM5.4). */
export function BuildLog(props: { core: CookCore }): VNode {
  const { core } = props;
  const [older, setOlder] = useState(false);
  const entries = [...core.log].reverse();
  const [latest, ...rest] = entries;
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="build-log">
      <h2 id="build-log" tabIndex={-1}>
        {COOK_COPY.buildLog}
      </h2>
      {latest === undefined ? (
        <p>{COOK_COPY.logEmpty}</p>
      ) : (
        <ol class="build-log">
          <LogEntryView entry={latest} core={core} />
          {older && rest.map((entry) => <LogEntryView key={entry.atMs} entry={entry} core={core} />)}
        </ol>
      )}
      {rest.length > 0 && (
        <button
          type="button"
          class="button"
          aria-expanded={older}
          onClick={() => setOlder(!older)}
        >
          {cookEarlier(rest.length)}
        </button>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
EOF

p='src/web/state/cook-hooks.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("import { useContext, useEffect } from 'preact/hooks';","import { useContext, useEffect, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';")
s=s.replace("import { useDispatch } from './hooks.js';","import type { UndoLabel } from './cook-slot.js';\nimport { announcementFor } from './cook-view.js';\nimport { useAppState, useDispatch } from './hooks.js';")
s=s.replace("""export function useCookEffects(): void {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();""","""export function useCookEffects(): void {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const cook = useAppState((state) => state.cook);

  // A rebuild, an undo or the end of dinner is announced once, when the state changes. The
  // first render is not a change, so a reload announces nothing (CM9.4).
  const previous = useRef(cook);
  useEffect(() => {
    const before = previous.current;
    previous.current = cook;
    if (cook === before) return;
    let label: UndoLabel | undefined;
    if (before?.undo != null && cook !== null && cook.undo === null && cook.core === before.undo.before) {
      label = before.undo.label;
    }
    const text = announcementFor(before, cook, label);
    if (text !== null) dispatch({ type: 'notice/set', text });
  }, [cook]);
""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
